    11-22-2013, 05:29 AM
    Hi there folks.

    Gwen’s player had to miss. We get a journal for her but it is not directly connected to the other three.

    Session XXXVI

    Doc’s Log

    Doc’s Log: 0435 Hours, Friday, June 4, 2010

    Oh God. I think Sebastian is the Terminator. I need slee


    Doc’s Log: 0930 Hours, Friday, June 4, 2010

    Well, that was classy. At least I didn’t leave any drool marks on the pages.

    It took me about an hour to center myself this morning, but when I did it was like I found a pool of perfectly still water within myself. I’m sure that’s got to mean something. Either way I’m feeling pretty good about the world. Given last night’s endurance test, I wasn’t sure I could move today. Oh well, time for work.


    Doc’s Log: 1345 Hours, Friday, June 4, 2010

    Nightshade came over with her picnic basket again for lunch. That is always so cool. She told me over lunch that she and Karen were going to go look for sites to open the 27 Club. She went out of her way to make sure I knew I was invited. It sounded like a great idea, given that this is an urge she’s sticking with, but I didn’t know how well Karen might take me tagging along. Plus I really did need to stick around and run the shop.

    Rose called just before Nightshade left. She was contacted by one of her friends from the coffee shop about the girl with a missing brother. We heard about this a few weeks ago, but nothing ever turned up or something like that. Anyway, there were more issues related to that and Rose wasn’t really confident she could talk to her without messing things up. She wanted me to come help, but I had just turned Nightshade down like 5 minutes before that.

    Fortunately, my girlfriend was in a good (and stable mood) so she offered to go with Rose to help give a sort of consultation. It sounds like there’s more going on with that now, and by more I mean Rose’s friend is offering to pay Ronin’s fees as an investigator to step in and help. That sounds like something is up to me. Guess we’ll see.


    Doc’s Log: 1410 Hours, Friday, June 4, 2010

    Holy shit. This possible new client of Ronin’s is hot. I know this because Nightshade sent me a picture over the phone. That woman is wonderful. Oh wow…I still have loyal customers, gotta go.


    Doc’s Log: 1605 Hours, Friday, June 4, 2010

    Nightshade came back after the meeting to fill me in. Might just be a parole bird flown the coop, but things don’t add up. Mom and Aunt don’t think he’s missing, but can’t say when he’s been around lately…Shatina can’t either but can see that everyone is saying the exact same story…word for word. Like someone else wrote it and then it was programmed into their heads. OR they are all in on it and the kid is more than just missing. We’re not sure yet, but I think Ronin will want a piece of this one.

    I’m not entirely sure Nightshade doesn’t want a piece of this one too, but likely in an entirely different way. I’d feel bad for considering that, but she did send me the interestingly cleavage angled pic.


    Doc’s Log: 2125 Hours, Friday, June 4, 2010

    So dinner was exciting as always. Snowflake was able to decipher and then engineer the binding rituals for the onyx box to work on Geists. He showed me his notes so I could learn it too, just in case. It’s important to use symbols that relate to the Geist’s theme or concept. Rose has offered the love letters Richard wrote her as ritual implements in trapping the Rose Bride. I will admit here that I never expected that. I think if this bitch were anything but a Geist, Rose would kill her with her own white-knuckled, blood stained, bare hands. That is more disturbing that it really should be for me. Anyway, I think I’ve figured out the main body of the ritual. Snowflake and I believe we can alter the rituals with some minor variations to trap other entities as well. We just haven’t taken the time to make those variations.

    Cupcake cleaned off the plates from dinner and then brought in the dress the girls brought in from the Wilson’s. It was finally time to check it out. It belonged to a Marjorie Crowe, who was shot for performing sexual acts on and with William Yarborough. She was killed by Phylls Eichmann, of the Eichmann called Wilson-Eichmanns. Phyllis sounds like she might have been prime Rose Bride material, but we haven’t found a link there yet. The dress is Marjorie’s anchor. Now if only we knew were the Deceased in the Blue Dress was, we’d be able to talk to her about it.

    Since we have no idea where she is, I decided that now as a great time to finally take a look at Rose’s arm. The first wound was already healed, but the gash from our second trip into the Underworld was still an issue for me. I gathered my supplies and we went back to her room so I could treat the wound properly. Surprisingly I haven’t had the chance to play Medic for awhile but it was much easier to slip into the role than I expected. That made me happy. That I managed to heal the wound with my treatment didn’t hurt my mood either.

    Meanwhile Nightshade pulled Chandra and Tavia into the ballroom to teach them the Time Warp. I hate I missed that, but healing Rose up was a tad more important. When I came back out, Nightshade was waiting. She needed to make sure I knew that Karen was insisting on somehow helping during the party. This will actually help with my idea to spotlight us when I lead Nightshade in the Gothic Waltz. Nightshade also made sure we knew that we needed someone to pretend to check IDs as well as real bartenders. I’m firmly in agreement about the bartenders.

    Ronin has been out all day but based on the plans we’ve made, when she gets back we’ll need some non-security-based cameras for footage usable during the Flesh Fair coming up. Now I’m going to go see what everyone else is up to tonight.


    Jasmine’s Diary

    June 4, 2010, Morning

    Training went well this morning. No so-called “Men in Black” showed up to bother us. Jean is making quite a bit of progress, which is good since we have a professional tryout competition in a few weeks.

    As I was preparing to leave campus, I received a call from Stanly, my friend from Lulu’s European Cafe. He was hoping I could put him in touch with Gwen. I thought she was in her office today, but when I called she didn’t answer. I think she had turned off her phone. In any event, I agreed to meet Stanly to see what he needed, as it apparently had to do with Shatina, his friend whose brother went missing a few weeks ago.

    I did call Doc to see if he could perhaps join me. I’m not the best “face” for the krewe (I think that’s the term Gwen has used), and wanted some help. Doc couldn’t get away from his shop, but Natalie was there and offered to join me. Although she and I don’t get along, she is very good at dealing with people, and often thinks of things I do not. I worried that we would end up arguing about something, but fortunately we didn’t and she provided some very useful insight during our talk with Shatina.

    We met Shatina and Stanly at Lulu’s. Her brother, Jamie, went missing a few weeks ago after being released from prison after serving a short sentence for trespassing (this after a longer drug-related sentence). However, Shatina realized that no one else seems to have noticed that he has disappeared, including her mother, grandmother and her brother’s friends (even his closest friend, who is called “Little Moe”). When she asked about it, Shatina found that everyone else simply assumes that he’s “been around,” and doesn’t worry about it, even though she found evidence that he’s been missing for some time now. It’s all very curious.

    Natalie wisely suggested that Jamie’s disappearance might be perfectly mundane. Perhaps he just fled his parole. But the circumstances are very odd, so I think there is more to it than that. We will need to find out where he was trespassing, and I think Officer Norris can help us with that. We may possibly visit Jamie’s friends as well.

    I did ask if we could get a strand or two of Jamie’s hair, something I can use with the Finding ceremony to locate him. My request must have sounded odd, so it’s a good thing Natalie was there to smooth things over. She explained why we needed the hair in mundane terms so that Shatina wouldn’t be upset by the strangeness of the request. I admit that I’ve grown so accustomed to supernatural goings-on that I sort of assumed Shatina knew were were paranormally inclined, so it did not even occur to me that she might find the request odd.

    But there’s something else odd about the situation as well. Once Shatina had left, Stanly told us that he was absolutely positive that Shatina had changed from being right-handed to left-handed. He has an eye for such things. It might seem like just an oddity, but given the other strange circumstances surrounding the disappearance of Shatina’s brother, I don’t think we can ignore it. Our krewe has seen too many supernatural happenings to discount such things.

    I haven’t a clue what’s going on with Shatina and her brother, but once Gwen gets home, we’ll talk to her about it and begin investigating. First off, though, we need to imprison the Rose Bride and resolve Sean Gleason.


    June 4, 2010, Afternoon

    We spent a good portion of the afternoon making preparations for trapping the Rose Bride. Simon managed to interpret and learn the DeCastro’s rituals, so we should be able to use them to imprison the Rose Bride. Our plan is to lure her into St. Brenden's church where her “brides” are anchored. Natalie will perform a protective warding ritual to help protect Simon while he enacts the ritual to seal the Rose Bride into the DeCastro Box. However, that will take time, and the rest of us must protect Simon and Natalie while also preventing the Rose Bride from leaving.

    I’ll be trying to make sure the Rose Bride is unable to escape by possessing my geist and physically immobilizing her as quickly as possible. I simply hope that I’m able to weather any retaliation she levels against me. I’m confident, but we have never dealt with anything this powerful before, so I’m also a bit anxious about it.

    In order to make the ritual more effective, we will need to use objects associated with romance, such as roses, bottles of wine, Valentines cards and such. I’ve offered to let Simon use some of my old love letters from Richard as well as our wedding rings, which should help to strengthen the ritual. We do not expect them to be damaged, so I should get them back safe and sound. But even if there were some risk, I would still offer them. The Rose Bride must be stopped.We simply cannot allow the Rose Bride to continue on as she has been, enslaving young lovers to false vows and destroying the sanctity of love simply to satisfy the selfish desires of her twisted petitioners. I will not allow her depredations to continue.

    June 4, 2010, Night

    With our preparations for tomorrow completed and Gwen still absent, we spent much of the evening planning for Bonnie-Lynn’s ball. Natalie, Alexia, and Simon know far more about hosting parties than the rest of us, so they did most of the planning. I’m a bit dismayed at the allowances they felt we should make for things like underage drinking and drug use, but I must confess that as such things are going to happen anyway, it’s best to contain them. That doesn’t mean I’m not perturbed at the thought that our guests might soil the beauty of Bonnie-Lynn’s special night with their drugs and sex and who knows what else, but there’s no way around it, so I might as well accept it.

    Before dinner, though, I asked Tavia to accompany me on a shopping trip for a new dress for the ball. She had wanted to spend some time with me, and it was a nice trip. I ended up with a conservative yet elegant black gown complete with a funereal veil, which I thought quite appropriate to the morbid character of Bonnie’s ball, even if it is a rather happy occasion for the ghosts. Natalie seemed to approve, anyway, which I will take as a good thing in this instance.

    After dinner the planning began in earnest. I won’t bother to record all of the various plans that were made. I barely remember all of them. I do know that Gwen is to be monitoring the party from her security station, and Chandra will be with her (Chandra cannot appear at the party without arousing the predatory instincts of the vampires who are certain to slip in among the goths). Alexia will be catering, Natalie will be the DJ and master of ceremonies, and Doc and Simon will be sort of the lords of the party (with Tavia on Simon’s arm, no doubt). As for me, I plan to simply move through the crowd, lurking and keeping an eye open for any trouble. We all plan to resort to our unique talents as sin-eaters to provide a bit of mysterious stage-craft to the night, which, I admit with some guilt, does sound like fun.

    I was pleased to hear that Natalie will be playing some music I like, such as The Cure, Collide, or even some swing music. I might find an opportunity to dance after all, though now that I think of it I doubt I’ll have anyone to dance with at the party. I suppose I could invite Jean, but I’m not really sure how I feel about encouraging that relationship, and the party doesn’t sound like his sort of thing at all. Moreover, he is sort of my connection to the “real” world, and I don’t know that I want to get him tangled up in a barely concealed supernatural event like this.


    June 4, 2010, Late Night

    I waited up until Gretchen returned home from her date with Richard. I had helped her prepare earlier, and gave her a pep talk then, but she was still anxious, of course. However, it seems her anxiety was misplaced. The date went very well as far as I could tell. They both seemed to enjoy it, and in fact Gretchen plans to go visit Richard’s place on Sunday night to watch some movies! I’m so happy that they’ve made a connection! I hope they continue to get on well. It warms my heart to see love blossom. I just can’t talk to Gretchen too long about it, lest she begin to give me a little too much information…


    Footnotes

    Prudish

    I’ve been thinking a lot about the reasons Natalie and I don’t get along lately. There are really many reasons, not the least of which is our views on love and relationships (Natalie is far too prone to assume that people are trying to use, control or manipulate her, for instance, when in fact she seems to be the one most prone to manipulate or control others…) but I suppose one of the key differences between us is the way she indulges her lusts while I seem to approach everything with a view toward caution.

    Upon reflection, I’m finding that I no longer have the same faith in true love that I once did, which worries the Drowned Bride. Perhaps my melancholy and loneliness have established too great a hold on me, but I no longer feel the same sense of certainty that I myself will find true love again. My certainty that others, such as Natalie and Doc, will succeed in romance and find happiness there remains strong. But my certainty that I myself am destined for another love like the one I’ve lost has wavered and, I fear, fallen into dust.

    Instead, I find my that I take refuge in prudence now more than ever before. I suppose that is why I am always trying to keep the krewe on task and why I constantly counsel care in our approach to our problems. It’s not that I’m being overly cautious (I’m not), but people like Natalie and Simon and Alexia, don’t always think before they act, and often run the risk of getting themselves in over their heads. Natalie is usually pretty careful when it comes to occult matters, but certainly not in regard to personal relationships. And Simon, in particular, worries me with his talk of using the Outsiders.

    Of course, a side effect of all of this is that the others think of me as a complete “prude” (as Gwen has put it) when it comes to relationships and sexual matters, and I suppose they do have a point. But that’s fine. I prefer being prudish to becoming entangled in unwise physical relationships, which I consider very serious and very precious indeed.

    The Blue Dress

    Alexia used her object-reading insights to examine the blue dress we found in the Wilsons’ attic, which we think is connected the confused ghost that ran through Albertus Magnus College two nights ago.

    She discovered that it belonged to a young woman named Marjorie Crowe. Marjorie was shot by Phyllis Eichmann over a man named William Yarborough, with whom Marjorie was intimately involved.

    The dress also seems to be Marjorie’s anchor, but she doesn’t seem to want to manifest around it, so it’s entirely possible she’s not aware that she’s a ghost

    Gwen’s Journal

    June 4, late night

    Went to the office this morning with every intention of staying there and keeping it open for more than two or three hours. I haven’t gotten any clients and it’s no wonder when the place is never flipping open! I mean, sure, I never expected to get many clients, but it was a good way to put myself to work and give people a way to find the krewe if we were needed. That’s less likely to happen if no one is ever there so I went in intending on being virtuous and do my continued explorations into low profile security systems from there. Two hours into the day I was playing Doom through this emulator I found online and considering that maybe, just maybe, I could find a better way to use my time.

    Enter Anders...yes, while my speakers were blaring with the sounds of shotgun firing and blood splattering he walked in and said he wanted to take me to lunch. Didn’t have to ask me twice. I finished the level and shut the game down. He didn’t have the limo, which I gotta admit, while it’s impressive, I don’t need to be impressed. His everyday car is perfectly fine - bit smoother ride than my Liberty, not that I’d ever give up. There was a picnic basket in the back seat and I figured we were going to the park or something - picnics are supposed to be romantic right? Instead we drive an hour and a half southwest to Sleepy Hallow onto the grounds of Kykuit, the Rockefeller Estate. OK, not gonna lie. I was impressed. Not only did he take me to a gorgeous place for a picnic and outdoor snuggle session, but he set up a meeting with the head of security for Kykuit. Before we left that night I’d gotten the backstage tour and had a much better idea of the security layout I wanted for the Bluffs.

    So yeah, total gushing entry tonight, but hey, I’m entitled sometimes - especially when my guy knocks one clean out the park. Anders turned off both our phones so Moiré couldn’t interfere in our date. I’ll head back to the Bluffs in the morning, I’m sure I managed to miss something important, but today was a pretty damn awesome day.

    End Session XXXVI

    The Prudish Entry reflects Jasmine’s Virtue Shift from Faith to Prudence, which has occurred for various reasons and was the decision of the player.

    Natalie sent pics of Shatina to Chandra and Doc. I included the message as she would have just sent the pic to doc. The pic for Shatina is…busty. Not why I picked it, but this was picked right up on. Also very pretty, but that is neither here nor there.

    Totally OOC and without commenting on correctness or not, Jasmine’s player’s theory is Shatina has been replaced by a Fetch, to explain her change in handedness. The jury is out on if she is aware she is a Fetch but it was pointed out if she is a Fetch that might cover why whatever effect was used on her family did not affect her. Also otherwise unnoted was “Little Moe” (so called because Jamie is a really big dude) found a set of Jamie’s cloths laying in his place, and Stanly in noting the whole left handed thing noted he was left handed and had an “evil eye” for spotting left handed people, and observed he had noted Natalie was left handed, and they had something of a bonding moment. They also chatted about rallies they had both been to, though Natalie intimated as much in her notes.

    We spent over an hour party planning IC. We had a sketch map and everything. Also, there was talk of DNAs, Designated Necking Areas, for the Goths. Chandra’s issues came up holistically in the discussion. Basically, if you have not read Vampire: The Requiem, Vampire’s Beasts react to other vampires, and in extreme cases can involve Frenzy. Chandra being a Penanggalan is not vulnerable to this in human form, but arouses it in other Vampires. Chandra has a power under Obfuscate to suppress the reaction, but again being a Penanggalan cannot use Disciplines in her human form. She can Obfuscate herself and be in flying head form, but if a Vampire with Auspex sees through that, he will also be affected by the stirring of his Beast. This also stacks onto the fact that Chandra is unpresented and has been living in the city for a bit. Vampires just have not had much reason to enter World of Wicker and have not noticed the head zipping around. Yet.

    Much like Sebastian, the events described by Gwen’s player she came up with and I gave the thumbs up on.

    ETA: since I was asked before Tavia has no romantic or physical interest in Jasmine. She just sees her as a kindred spirit in some ways and wants to be closer friends.

    Hope you enjoyed, comments or questions welcome.

        OK here we go. Big fight this session, our 3rd real battle, not counting throwing the monster off the car that one time. Just as rough as the row with the werewolves, too. Alright here we go

        Session XXXVII

        Doc’s Log

        Doc’s Log: 0320 Hours, Saturday, June 5, 2010

        I went on patrol tonight. It was weird, not because I was out patrolling again, but because I was out on a Friday night.

        Everyone pretty much scattered to do their own thing after the party planning. Nightshade seemed very distracted and distant. That plus not seeing Karen around told me that I should give her some space even though I wanted to try and help. I fought those thoughts down and kept to myself. By around midnight, there was no screaming, no sobbing, and no Ronin, so I decided to head out. The streets were quiet, which is never a bad thing so I came in just a little early. Assuming Ronin is back with us, tonight will be huge. Need to rest up.


        Doc’s Log: 1450 Hours, Saturday, June 5, 2010

        This has been a busy Saturday. No laying around resting up for the fight with a Geist working for a Kerberos. Nope, we’ve got things to do.

        Eh, I would have been bored anyway.

        Meditation was unsurprisingly more difficult today. After an hour and a half, I was finally at peace again. I knew of all things, this was not something I could pass on today. I have no idea what I may be forced to do tonight and I’ll need to keep everything together no matter what.

        I came down a little bit late for breakfast with the others. That’s still a new concept for me anyway, but Rose surprised me this morning. Always the early riser, she answered the door this morning for a young man that seems to have picked up Marjorie Crowe hitchhicking in front of the House last night. He was pretty spooked because after she asked him to take her to Henderson Street, she disappeared. I didn’t hear him tell his tale, but most ghost stories like that go about the same way. They usually have the woman stay very quiet on the ride home, then when the kind gentleman gets out to go around and open the door for her, she’s gone. Now in this case, we know Marjorie’s anchors aren’t close enough for her to get to Henderson Street, so at some point she was just sucked back out of the car. Not sure if he believes he got to her house with her still in the car or not. But according to Rose, he was really shaken up. She was confused when she opened the door and he jumped. As pale as she is, I asked her if she was standing where sunlight could hit her. That confused her but she said no. I figured the poor kid thought she was another ghost in the same creepy house. And while funny, Rose told him that this happened sometimes and not to worry about it. While that was probably a good time to lie about something, it’s unusual for Rose to do it or to pull it off. Apparently the kid believed it as he just kinda walked backward off the porch staring at her til he got back in his car.

        Now what this tells us is that Rose was really mean to a frightened teenager (and as terrible people we all laughed about it). Also that Marjorie doesn’t realize she’s dead. Henderson Street is close to the Eichmann called Wilson’s neighborhood. We can also determine with some educated guesses that Marjorie has probably tried (and maybe succeeded in) going home before. Either her anchor wasn’t close enough or home just didn’t seem like home any more (it’s only been what, 70 years). The theory is that this traumatized her enough that she needed to return to her anchors to sleep. During her sleep, she forgot that she’d either gone home and it was different or tried and got jerked back, so she continues trying to get home. Ronin came in and grabbed some toast as we were talking about all this. I pointed out, before what I knew was coming hit, that Snowflake, Ronin, or Cupcake would need to try and explain things to her once we could get her out of her anchor. Nightshade looks too spooky and possibly too loose for a well groomed lady of 30’s society to talk to, while Rose and I probably aren’t white enough to make her comfortable. Yes I’m stereotyping. The idea is to keep her comfortable. Then again, this is a woman who was killed in a flapper dress for getting “tangled up” with a man another woman wanted. She might make an offer to me and Nightshade. Who knows? Now it was time for everyone (Rose) to give Ronin a hard time for not answering her phone. I threw in a jab for fun, but apparently Anders swooped in and carried her off to some picturesque garden estate complete with its own invisible security system. So she had a romantic picnic dinner and then got to tour a state of the art facility with an emphasis on their security installations and how to make it invisible. I tell ya what. Anders is definitely making it hard on the rest of us.

        Rose filled Ronin in on the Johnson Missing Persons case. It’s weird because this is Ronin’s first paying case, she wasn’t there to accept it, someone else is paying for it, and no one else believes the target is missing. I’ve seen worse series premieres on TV though. Then Ronin kinda snapped. I knew bits and pieces of her past as she knows it. She had a little brother named Jamie that got into trouble with the police too. After he got out, he disappeared too but that was intentional since Ronin put him in to start with. Still, that detail didn’t matter so much. Right then for Ronin, it became personal.

        In her mental day planner, Ronin decided right then she would call the parole officer on Monday, then she called Chuck. Officer Norris not only knew about the situation but was already worried about it. He told Ronin that the parole officer gave him the same kind of story about Jamie being around, just couldn’t say where right then. Because Chuck is a professional, he asked to see the meeting records...and the parole officer started entering them right then, saying it must have just slipped his mind to do it before then. So there were no records of Jamie being there for at least a month. Chuck also said when he talked to Alice, Shatina’s mom, that she and her sister both thought something was weird about Shatina. The grandmother didn’t mention anything about it, but Stanly’s weird observation about her switching from left to right handedness suddenly held a little more importance.

        Ronin had Rose call and setup another meeting with Shatina at Lulu’s European Coffee. Ronin thought that she could learn something from seeing Shatina’s aura. Shatina had already found a sample of Jamie’s hair so this was a great excuse to meet again. I was at the House with Ronin and Nightshade at the time. Rose and Snowflake were checking out St. Brendan’s so Rose would already know the best places to hide and possibly from which to ambush. Cupcake was out shopping for romantically symbolic items (roses, box of chocolates, etc) for the ritual. She had Chandra along for other romantic purposes, I’m sure. We left them to their shopping while Ronin drove Nightshade and me out to Lulu’s. Snowflake stayed with Rose and they both waited in the coffee shop for our “client.” When Shatina got there, Ronin scanned her soul and it was weird. She couldn’t say what was wrong but her face reminded me of the soldiers that walked past while I had both hands wrist-deep in a body. The urge to puke was there, but the mind bending anguish of trying to understand the sight was winning out. When I asked Ronin if she was okay, she just said of Shatina, “That ain’t right.” Not exactly what I was asking, but with Ronin, you take what you can get sometimes. Apparently the more normal parts of the girl’s aura proved that she was scared and excited, which is completely understandable given the circumstances. But there was something else that Ronin just couldn’t verbalize in any way other than wrong. I started to get out, but she grabbed my arm and told us to wait.

        I don’t like dealing with something when we don’t know anything about it. We watched through the windows from the parking lot as our little feint played out. Shatina brought a brush that had some of Jamie’s hair and was very excited to hear that we were looking into things for her. Ronin kept watch on her the entire time while Rose and Snowflake played the smiling faces. Ronin checked her aura again at some point and the emotions changed appropriately, but she still got that bizarre, brain-twitching “wrong” from it. None of us know or have even heard of something that could rewrite auras like that, so this falls totally into the “Other” category. When they came out, Rose wanted to do the Finding ceremony so we could get on Jamie’s trail. Oddly, she had a real problem picking anything up on the mystic radar. So much so, I went in to Lulu’s and bought more coffee so they wouldn’t get weird about us camping in their parking lot. After what might have been two hours, Rose got frustrated and we all headed back to the House. She tried again there, but she still couldn’t seem to get a lock on Jamie. We’re not sure what’s going on, but Rose decided we’d need to finish with the Rose Bride before she tried again. So what we expected to be either a simple dead body or a manhunt has turned into one more mysterious item on our ever growing Mystic To-Do list.

        Now I need to go so we can start preparing to go desecrate an altar. Mom will be so proud.


        Doc’s Log: 1845 Hours, Saturday, June 5, 2010

        Snowflake and I discussed Tavia going to St. Brendan’s to help with the ritual. Tavia was there to make the ritual go smoother. I was the backup in case something happened to Snowflake. We all headed down into the newly opened GMC room to cast a magic spell. Okay yes, that looks just as dumb written down as when they say it on TV. I got various images together from around the House of people that were casting spells. I figured if they were pictured in this place, they must be good at it. Then I started the ritual. After an hour, I granted Tavia all the luck I could. Snowflake was of course much better at this than me, so he granted himself the luck. Then we came back upstairs feeling better about our chances.

        The plan is to take Karen and Tavia with us to the church. With Karen standing inside the ritual area, Snowflake will draw two different warding circles. He and Tavia will stay in one and make sure the ritual goes off, with Cupcake inside as melee protection, you know with all the arms. The other circle will be Nightshade, Ronin, and me. Nightshade will be dealing with the ghostly Bopsy Twins once the altar beating starts. Ronin and I will be around for ranged support. I’m also the one likely to leave the circle to help in any other location or capacity. Rose will be hiding in shadows until the Rose Bride shows up. Then Rose is going to swoop in and grab the Rose Bride in Twilight while Snowflake completes the ritual and we suck her into the box.

        The first complication came up when Nightshade told us Karen wanted to stay. I know Nightshade was having some issues with this as Karen was only included because she’s a normal mortal. This will make creating the warding circles easier, but having her around during the actual binding will not be helpful. I stepped in to try and make this easier on everyone by offering to give Karen a spare ear curl. That way she could keep completely aware of what was happening without being there. That seemed like a simple compromise to me. Nightshade grumbled but accepted the curl. The next issue was a little more touchy. Snowflake asked if we could have a minor blood drive for his rituals. The circles needed to be drawn in Sin-Eater blood, freely given and mixed with chalk. Both circles would require more blood than Snowflake could give and still be at his peak mental ability. Nightshade, Ronin, and Rose all stepped up to volunteer their blood to assist, so I went and got my equipment. Draining each of the four of enough blood to make the circles didn’t require anyone to be injured and we still got our ritual materials. All hail the power of medicine.

        Because we are going into a church after hours and damaging things, I made sure that everyone had gloves on. I don’t care what the movies say about supernatural creatures, we are still human enough to leave prints. I didn’t mention it to anyone but I added a few bottles of ammonia to my pack to disrupt any blood we might accidentally leave behind. I do not need any of us under investigation by the police. Before we left, Nightshade conducted her Blessings of the Horsemen ceremony, and it was very comforting to remember that we were acting as their Will.

        If I make it out of this, I’ll talk about other stuff...like how scared I am and trivial shit like that. Oh good, Nightshade has Clyde dressed as a homeless guy so looks like we’re almost ready. Next stop Murder Ballads for Rose to top off her reserves before stormin’ St. Brendan’s to bind a Geist in a box.


        Doc’s Log: 0520 Hours, Sunday, June 6, 2010

        FUCK! Only because that bitch is a Geist is she allowed to continue her existence! I’m writing this now because my patients are resting as comfortably as I can make them. And I need something to keep me from going off the deep end and ripping the Rose Bride out of that box to kick her ass myself! I’ll try to calm down and make this as detailed as I can remember it.

        St. Brendan’s is a nice enough looking place from the outside. Inside was another story all together. The pale light of the moon shining through the stained glass tinted everything crimson. The inside of a Catholic church all bathed in crimson light is NOT the place to take a date on Saturday night. Unless that date is Nightshade. But that’s not why we were here.

        Snowflake and Karen got down to business casting the circles. I moved off into the shadows to prepare and observe. In Twilight, I noticed eight Jealousy spirits beginning to take interest in several of us. They all looked like women with very exaggerated figures, fishnet stockings, very little other clothes, and bright green eyes. Four stereotypically cherubic Cupids let me know there were Love spirits drifting between the couples as well. But the Faceless Bride, which I was desperately hoping was a Marriage spirit, stood out from the rest. She seemed to belong in a way the others just could not. I tried to talk to her but she just looked very...confused I guess is the right word for someone without a face. Rose spoke to her in “Vow-Speak” and asked her to help us stop the Rose Bride from corrupting the very concept she embodied. She seemed to agree...at least we thought so. She moved to Tavia and whispered something to her, but Tavia glanced up and spoke in some kind of language I’ve never heard before and the spirit stepped back in surprise. Just before we had everything together, the spirit spoke to Karen before we could get her out. Then there was a conversation with Nightshade which did not go well. Karen left but had the curl so she kept us updated on her whereabouts. That made me feel better, but given what I’d just seen, I knew Nightshade was not completely okay...and again there was nothing I could do about it. I moved into the shadow of an alcove to set my body down while I stepped into that of the Dog of War. Knowing that I was going to be in a spiritual fight, I then unlocked my Stigmata Shroud. The Mask of the Red Death costume wrapped itself around my canine form just as well as my human form, even down to the skull face. Now I was ready.

        Walking out of the shadows, I doubt my Krewe expected the addition of my Shroud but no one reacted. The circle was a little cramped given my size, so I turned sideways between Ronin and Nightshade to await the inevitable chaos. Nightshade set Clyde on the altar and we watched and waited while the zombie pummeled the heavy finished wood. We didn’t have long to wait as the Cheshire Girls didn’t waste any time coming to protect their anchor. Nightshade slashed her arm twice with her ritual dagger and used the power of her blood to command the ghosts. Obviously not willing to stand down, the Cheshire Girls attempted to control Clyde’s mind. Clyde, being a zombie, didn’t react and kept wailing on their anchor.

        Then shit got real.

        The Rose Bride appeared and Clyde burst into angry red flames. The Faceless Bride rushed the Geist and though I don’t think it was very effective, she got a good swing in. Then Ronin shot the Rose Bride a look as cold as the desert night and the Geist seemed to just freeze up. Rose swooped in and grabbed the Rose Bride so I did as well. I figured the more of us holding her the less danger to the group. I won’t make that mistake again. As soon as the Rose Bride was grappled, two winged female torsos appeared. Okay minions of Our Lady were not immediately expected. In the split second I wasn’t looking at the Rose Bride, she blasted me with her red fire of hate. That made it hard to see, no way anyone could overlook me, and it stung a bit. Then one of those legless Nike statues lit me again. Next thing I know, one of the Cheshire Girls is trying to pull my strings. I was getting it from all sides. I snarled at the Nike wannabe and took a bite out of the Cheshire Girl. I glanced over just in time to see the Faceless Bride bitch slap the Rose Bride hard enough to knock her head to the side. I was glad the Jealousy spirits ran when the fight started. The Cheshire Girl tried to get inside my head again, but this time Cupcake stepped out of the warding circle with all of her hands tightening into fists. She beat that ghost’s ass like nothing I’ve seen in a long time. So after watching Cupcake wipe the floor with one of the Cheshire Girls, I took a bite out of the other. The whole time I was getting blasted with more Hatefire from both the Nike ripoffs. I guess Ronin decided she’d had enough of that because I heard something that I hope was Welsh come out of her mouth the same time sickly green Balefire consumed the winged torso. It dropped to the floor, but it didn’t go limp like it was dead so I guess it was just knocked out. Seeing its “sister” take a beating, the other Nike statue flew higher so it was out of Ronin’s visual range. Cheap bitch. Cupcake finished off the other Cheshire Girl as I got blasted yet again from that winged copycat. Then the Faceless Bride turned and screamed like some kind of Banshee, blasting the torso out of the church. The Rose Bride got one final shot off on Rose and she made it count. The Hatefire engulfed Rose and I could immediately smell burning flesh when the Rose Bride screamed as she was jerked away from Rose and into the DeCastro Box. Snowflake and Tavia did it! I was about to get really happy until I heard Rose collapse on the floor. Ronin grabbed the altar cloth and helped put her out. I realized I was still on fire too, but I just rolled around til it went out.

        Now I’m reliving more triage moments, so I’ll have to wait until I calm down again to finish this off.


        Jasmine’s Diary

        Saturday, June 5, 2010, Morning

        I was meditating this morning when someone rang the mansion’s rather ominous-sounding doorbell. I was the only one awake at the time, so I answered the door to find a nervous young man named Robert Adams there. I seemed to make him uneasy for some reason. He asked if anything bad had happened at the house recently, such as a death in the family or something, and then told me that he had picked up a hitchhiker in a blue dress the night before, but that she had simply disappeared from his car before he could deliver her to her home on Henderson Street. He was quite shaken by the experience.

        The hitchhiker matched the description of Marjorie, the ghost we seem to have awakened by removing the blue dress from the Wilsons’ attic. It sounds as though she does not know she is dead and is trying to make her way home, but is being pulled back by the weight of her anchor.

        I tried to reassure Robert by lying and telling him that this wasn’t the first time the ghost had hitched a ride, and that she was harmless, if a bit spooky. He seemed to accept that, but was still quite shaken. I supposed if I weren’t so accustomed to dealing with the dead, I would have been shaken by the experience too.

        I told the others about this over breakfast, and Alexia got out the blue dress and placed it on a dress mannequin in an attempt to gain Marjorie’s attention. But apparently the trauma of being snapped back by her anchor made her temporarily dormant again and she isn’t responding to us. We plan to take a drive along the road in the coming nights to see if we can’t find her hitchhiking again. If we pick her up, maybe we can explain her situation to her.


        Saturday, June 5, 2010, Afternoon

        Gwen returned home in the morning, and I filled her in on the details of the Shatina case. I also chided her a bit on disappearing yesterday. I must have come off as somewhat annoyed, which I suppose I was, but Gwen took it in her typical good humor.

        She decided to call her friend Clevon to ask whether Shatina’s brother Jamie has checked in with his parole officer lately, and discovered that the parole officer has had a similar experience to Shatina’s family. The officer had no record that Jamie had been showing up for his parole meetings (implying that Jamie had not, in fact, shown up), but he thought he remembered meeting with Jamie, even though he couldn’t recall any details of the meetings.

        We decided to go ahead and see if we couldn’t track Jamie down with the Finding ritual, so I called Shatina and set up a meeting at Lulu’s so that I could get the strand of Jamie’s hair we discussed during our first meeting.

        Simon accompanied me into the meeting this time, but Gwen, Doc, and Natalie waited in a car to watch. Gwen read Shatina’s aura from afar and found that her aura felt wrong…very wrong. But she’s not sure why, because otherwise Shatina seemed very sincere and hopeful and even, perhaps a bit obsessed with finding her brother. It sounded as though Shatina is sincere, but there may be something wrong with her that she doesn’t even know about. I find that quite troubling.

        Gwen said the strangeness of Shatina’s aura looked different than it would look if someone were mind-controlling or possessing her. She really doesn’t think that vampires, ghosts or spirits of any sort are involved, so that doesn’t leave us with many options. I suppose I’ll have to ask Mongler about it on Monday. Maybe it has to do with his people, but I really doubt it since I couldn’t see anything odd about Shatina, and normally I see Changeling things as they are.

        Before leaving the cafe, I also asked Stanly what he knew about his uncle William Yarborough. Stanly thought very well of him, and also of his wife, Phyllis Yarborough (previously Phyllis Eichmann). According to him, she is a very kind old woman, and he is quite fond of her. However, we know that it was Phyllis who shot and killed Marjorie over William’s affections. Naturally, I didn’t reveal any of that to Stanly. There’s no sense in upsetting him with a truth best left hidden. But we may need to approach Phyllis over the issue in order to resolve Marjorie’s ghost.

        After Shatina was gone, I tried using the Finding ceremony to track down Jamie, but it wasn’t working for me. It’s always harder to find someone alive rather than dead, so that’s encouraging at least. Still, I had to give it up after an hour or two. We’ll try again tomorrow.


        Saturday, June 5, 2010, Evening

        We investigated the St. Brenden’s Church earlier today to plan our confrontation with the Rose Bride. Now Simon, Natalie, and Doc are preparing the wards. Natalie has already performed a ceremony to bless our endeavors. The Drowned Bride is giving me strength right now; the Rose Bride is an enemy to everything we believe, and we won’t stop until she’s been put away.


        Monday, June 7, 2010, Noon

        I’m feeling a bit better now, though still in a great deal of pain. I need to write about what happened Saturday night, though, so I’ll try to rally my strength and do.

        As we finished preparations for our trap, I possessed the Drowned Bride to assist me in fighting the Rose Bride. Upon doing so, I noticed quite a few spirits lurking in the church sanctuary amidst the bloody glow of the moonlight shining through the red-tinted stained glass windows. There were several jealousy spirits flitting between Simon and Karen, a few minor love spirits paying attention to Doc, Natalie, Karen and Gwen, and a faceless bride who seemed drawn to Karen and Tavia.

        I knew immediately that the faceless bride was a marriage spirit, and I felt a certain connection to her. I was a bit unhappy, though, to see her whispering into Karen’s mind. Karen was, of course, completely unaware of the spirit’s influence, but was nevertheless affected by it, and seemed to decide that she needed to talk to Natalie about something immediately in private. I wanted to stop her, because I knew she was going to ask Natalie about marriage, and that would no doubt upset Natalie and distract her from the task at hand. But with the other preparations and spirits to deal with, I couldn’t intervene. And, as I suspected, Natalie looked a bit dazed when they returned.

        Meanwhile, I spoke to the marriage spirit and asked her to help us in the upcoming fight, explaining the way the Rose Bride perverted the very thing the spirit stood for. It was not easy communicating with her, since she could only understand communication phrased in the language of marriage vows, but thanks a bit to my kinship with her, I eventually I got her to understand, and she agreed to help.

        Doc manifested his Stigmata Marionette, and Alexia donned her Phantasmal Caul, growing additional arms which moved constantly and gracefully at her sides. I sank into the shadows of my hiding spot clad in my Tear-Stained Caul and Primeval Shroud, which I used to grant myself strength well beyond that of a mortal human. We had the zombie, which Natalie has named Clyde, attack the altar to draw the attention of Sean’s Gleason’s brides. They arrived very quickly, and we attacked. Doc, Alexia and Gwen focused their attentions on them while the brides tried unsuccessfully to take control of the zombie’s mind. Natalie worked her magic to weaken the brides, and then the Rose Bride showed up.

        Two Underworld ghost-spirits resembling angel-winged, feminine torsos also appeared to help the Rose Bride. I’ve decided to call them Angelic Venuses. They were servants of Our Lady, so I think it is safe to assume that we have earned Our Lady’s attention. Doc and the others concentrated on fighting the brides and the Venuses, and over the course of the battle they suffered a few injuries, particularly Natalie. Doc managed to interpose himself between the Rose Bride and Simon to prevent her from lashing out at him with Pyre-Flame until I could maneuver her away from the warding circle, and Simon worked madly to complete the ritual and seal the Rose Bride into the DeCastro box. The marriage spirit, meanwhile, helped us attack our enemies, and even unleashed a terrible scream that helped to dispel the Angelic Venuses. Aside from that, though, I don’t really remember any of the details of the fight because I was focused on the Rose Bride.

        When she arrived, I slipped from cover and locked her in a hold, using all of my tai chi and wushu skills to maintain it despite her immense strength. The Tear-stained Caul allowed my strength to flow in tune with her struggles, matching even the slightest of her movements to keep her completely immobile. I’m fortunate that she made the mistake of trying to attack Simon once more before I immobilized her and turned her away from him. If she had tried harder to escape before I was able to fully cement my hold on her, she might have slipped free. But once I had her locked in my hold, she could barely move as I drove her slowly to ground.

        But in her desperate attempts to escape, she unleashed the full fury of her rage upon me. Again and again my body erupted with her rage-filled flames, and each time the flames were extinguished by my shroud and the cold waters of my Caul, leaving me wreathed in scalding steam.

        Simon finally finished the ritual, and the Rose Bride was torn screaming from my grasp into the DeCastro box. And then I collapsed, covered with severe burns from her flames. Were I still mortal, I would have been dying. As it was, I was simply in excruciating pain. And yet even that faded in the face of my utter focus. I had withstood her fury and kept her at bay long enough for us to trap her. We’ve stopped her from perverting or destroying any more romances.

        Thanks to Doc’s medical attention and my supernatural fitness as a sin-eater, I’m healing rather quickly, but it will still be several days before I’m in tip-top shape again. I’ve been confined to bed since the battle. The pain is terrible, but it is mitigated somewhat by the knowledge that I was instrumental in keeping the Rose Bride from escaping. And perhaps even more importantly, my efforts managed to keep her from hurting the other members of the krewe. I’ll gladly suffer such wounds again to protect them.


        Footnotes

        Pale as a Ghost

        When I met Robert Adams at the door of Darkhaven Bluffs, he was already nervous, but I could tell that his anxiety increased when he saw me, even though I don’t consider myself to be particularly intimidating. I suppose the house helped; it’s very spooky, especially in the early morning mists.
        I mentioned it later and Gwen said that he probably thought I was a ghost too at first because I’m so pale.
        I didn’t used to be so pale. Before I died I had a nice complexion. But ever since I was possessed by the Drowned Bride, the sun doesn’t really take to me, and I’m almost white as a sheet. I wonder if it has anything to do with the lightless ocean depths in which the Bride dwelt for so many decades before becoming a geist?
        It would be nice to get my color back, but I don’t think I ever will. Natalie is apparently jealous since she has to work to remain as pale as she does, which seems quite backward to me. But then again, most of what Natalie does seems backward to me.

        American Culture and Idioms

        The Highlander
        As we were discussing the various possible causes for the disturbing qualities in Shatina’s aura, Gwen made some comment to the effect of “Vampires? Werewolves? What’s next? Highlanders?” Naturally, I was confused why she would equate the Scottish with supernatural creatures, but it turns out she was referring to a particular movie involving immortal supernatural beings called “Highlanders.” They apparently cut each others’ heads off to gain more power. It sounded excessively violent for my tastes, but I’m not surprised Gwen is a fan.

        Poe

        Doc’s Stigmata Shroud manifestation resembles the Dog of War clad in the garb of the Red Death from Edgar Allen Poe’s story “The Masque of the Red Death” (a very odd-looking beast, to be sure). Yes, I’ve read quite a bit of Poe, given my occult interests, but I read most of it after becoming a sin-eater. Unlike many who read Poe, I prefer his romantic work, which is hardly surprising. I can very much identify with the emotions of grief and loss conveyed in poems like “The Raven” and “Annabel Lee,” and while I don’t treasure them as much as I do my Keats, they are still quite precious to me.

        Gwen’s Journal

        June 5, late

        First off - successful day, nobody died so bonus there. Went after Rosie tonight, broke into Sean’s Brides’ church and Nat’s zombie completely desecrated the altar. I know it was for a good cause, but isn’t that some kind of major sin? Well, the zombie’s nothing but a meat suit at this point anyway so maybe it doesn’t matter, but I’m still a little worried about that. The Angel says we’ll be forgiven as it was done for the greater good - well, he’s the expert right? And it did work. As soon as Clyde (maybe it was Boris - I can’t remember what Nat calls the zombie I’ll have to ask) started whaling on the altar the Brides showed up. Nat cut herself a couple of times to take control of the Brides - she didn’t get total control of them, but she damaged them enough that Rosie showed up which was the plan all along.

        She focused her rage on Clyde (Brutus?) and he burst into flame but didn’t stop hitting the altar. Her rage manifests through pyreflame unlike mine which can be one seriously powerful burst of emotion or it could just force all sorts of bodily fluids out of your body - I can work with either of them so go me. While Rosie was focused on Clyde (Gomer?) I shot a good dose of Passion rage at her and managed to overwhelm her emotions for a second.

        Everyone kept telling me I couldn’t kill her as that would just send her back to Our Lady of the Desperate Housewives so I backed off after that. Jazz grabbed her as Simon started his ritual to suck her into the demon box. Apparently it was working because she tried to attack Simon rather than get out of Jazz’s hold. Doc got in between her and Simon so he took the attack instead of our Warlock (would Simon consider that a compliment or an insult, I’m not really sure.) Doc was both impressive, and really silly looking during the fight. His shroud is well...theatrical. Intimidating as hell I’m sure, but even though I know it’s armor and draws on the power of the blood, puffy sleeves, a pimp hat is a bit ridiculous. The Skull mask is pretty awesome, and since he’d possessed the Dog of War that skull was in the shape of a dog and yeah, that was disturbing. Once he said something about his shroud resembling the Mask of the Red Death.

        During all this a couple of torsos with wings seemed to appear ready to come to Rosie’s aid. Now they backed away from the protection circles Simon cast before we started the fight but they focused on Doc since he was standing outside the circles. Passion Rage is a wonderful thing and I hammered one of the Feather Torsos clean out. The other torso was scared off by the marriage spirit Jazz talked into working with us. I gotta come up with a better name for them. As servants of Our Lady we’ll be dealing with them again I’m sure.

        Alexia took out the Brides and they went back into their anchor, the altar. Simon finished the ritual fairly quickly, but not before Jazz was burned to a crisp. When Rosie couldn’t hurt Simon right away she’d turned her ire on Jazz trying to get her to let her go I think. But at least now Rosie’s stuck in the demon box and she won’t be causing any damage near as we can tell. Doc’s been treating Jazz’s wounds since we got back, I’m pretty sure she’s going to be out of commission for a few days. But she’ll be OK, of that we’re pretty confident.

        Oh yeah - I almost forgot, I did miss some pretty important stuff yesterday when Anders kidnapped me. It seems I have a client. This is a good thing, but the case itself is flippin’ weird. Jazz mentioned about a month ago a friend of hers from Lulu’s knew someone that’s brother had gone missing. Well she passed my card on to him then but nothing came of it until yesterday. Seems the situation’s continued and gotten a bit strange. Everyone but Shatina says they’ve seen her brother (who’s name is Jamie and just out of prison - WTH? Are all little brothers named Jamie criminals?). But he’s never around when she tries to find him. Since no one could get a hold of me yesterday - and dude Jazz gave me a talking to about that, ha! - Jazz and Nat did the preliminary interview with her. She’s already talked to Chuck who couldn’t meet up with him but since everyone else said they’d seen him there was no case. Jazz wanted to set up a meeting immediately but I refused. I don’t want to talk to this woman until I can talk to the kid’s parole officer and find out if he’s been showing up for his meetings. Considering Chuck’s involved I’m pretty sure the parole officer’s a dead end, he would’ve talked to him already. But I’d prefer to talk to the dude myself, y’know? What’s kind of strange about the whole thing is when they met up yesterday Jazz’s friend Stanley was there too and he noticed formerly right handed Shatina was now a southpaw. You know, the dominant hand generally doesn’t change especially out of the blue. So yeah, this was our case and I needed to get busy.

        First thing I did was call Chuck to get his take on it and find out what the parole officer said. THAT CRAZY FOOL TAMPERED WITH HIS RECORDS! In front of Chuck for godssake! It seems the Parole Officer said the same thing as everyone else - same wording I mean. That’s just weird. But Chuck told me he thinks Shatina’s in danger - there’s nothing pointing to it but his gut says she’s marked for something. I’ve looked through my journals and me and Chuck have talked enough I know to pay attention to his instincts.

        I told Jazz to set up a meeting with Shatina - she wanted to get some of Jamie’s hair so she could do a finding ritual on him. When they met I stayed out in the car with Doc and Nat. I just wanted to see the girl and get a read on her so I knew what I was dealing with. There’s something seriously weird about her aura. Gave me the heebie-jeebies like you wouldn’t believe! But I couldn’t see anything strange. Just a weird skin crawly feeling.

        Jazz also had the chance to talk to Stanley about a relative of his, William Yarbough. For a refresher, that’s the dude the girl in the blue dress was killed over. Seems her name was Marjorie and now she’s hitching rides trying to go home. Sad, none of us thinks she realizes she’s dead. Anyway, Wiliam Yarbough turns out to be Stanley’s great uncle, and get this - he married Marjorie’s murderer Phyllis. William’s dead but Phyllis is still alive and kicking at 98.

        One other thing while we were laying the trap for Rosie there were some spirits hanging around the church. Some jealousy spirits, a couple of minor love spirits, and a marriage spirit. I couldn’t see any of this but Jazz could and so could Doc as they’d possessed their geists for the fight. Seems the jealousy spirits were interested in Simon and Karen (she was there to help cast the circles), the love spirits were hanging around Karen, Doc, and Nat...me too, but I’m really not interested in thinking about that right now. And then the marriage spirit - that one that Doc & Jazz talked into working with us - was really interested in Tavia (there to help Simon with the ritual) and Karen.

        Either the jealousy or the marriage spirit got to Karen because she picked the worst time in the world to push Nat for more of a commitment. I’m not too happy about that on a couple of levels. I want Nat to be happy, yes, but this triangle she’s got going with Karen and Doc has barely been in business for a week now. Doc hasn’t really had the chance to show how awesome he could be for Nat. She seems happy with him for the most part. I know she’s got feelings for him and for Karen - if that little girl thinks she can push for more and not cause problems with Nat and Doc I think she’s sorely mistaken. So - the chick’s making demands that could potentially hurt Doc’s happiness and she rattled our necromancer right before a big fight. Yeah, I’m not happy with her.

        I think that’s all I can really recall right now. Figured I’d be ready to sleep but not so much. Think I’ll go get started on that research I need to do.

        End Session XXXVII

        As an aside, we ended as soon as the fight did, as it was slightly past ending time. The ahead projections were for story purposes since, say, Jasmine would not have written as soon as she got back.

        Jasmine's player had awful luck with the Finding Ritual, rolling several times but garnering little success. Eventually they gave up as they had to get ready for the confrontation.

        I ruled they could attempt to communicate with the Marriage Spirit using phrases from local weddings to string together sentences. I let them roll to do it but gave extra dice for the players stringing lines together.

        Jasmine took, all told, 18 health levels. She built her health levels up to 11 from her natural 9 with Shroud and Caul, and Bulwarked 7. She managed to size the Rose Bride and turn her away from her allies, so she was the only target for the Rose Bride’s attacks. Doc took like 4 Health but Bulwarked all of it, and Natalie took 2 lethal from activating the Stigmata Marionette. Simon has the Warding Circle spell that he used, as opposed to Natalie’s Warding the Household (which was too big to be useful there). Natalie, Gwen and (initially) Doc were in one circle while Simon, Alexia, and Tavia were in the other and Jasmine hid (Karen was only there long enough to give a bonus for Circle Creation).

        I should also note that dealing with the Rose Bride with the demon box was a good idea, but the Rose Bride/Bonnie Lynn/etc plot was written independently of the DeCastro plot. I did not have in mind specifically how they would deal with the Rose Bride, but as they had the box the intelligent choice became obvious.

        For various reasons, we wound up running the next session early. We covered the rest of Saturday night as well as Sunday, Monday, and Tuesday, the 6th-8th. So the next session will begin with Saturday night again and cover three more days. I have no idea if the journals for that will come earlier or not as a result. The current long term plan is cover the party, cover the aftermath if any, then skip to the Flesh Fair for like a session, then move ahead some times, say a month and a half or so give some time for stuff to change.

        Hope you enjoyed, comments and questions welcome.
