            Session XXIX

            Doc’s Log

            Doc’s Log: 1345 Hours, Sunday, June 6, 2010

            Wow. I’m really not use to sleeping this late, but then I think I might have pushed myself a little far last night. Still, I wish I could remember whatever it was I was dreaming about.

            Looking back over my last entry, my mind was really scattered this morning. I know I pulled 26 hours without sleep and medic duty before but I’ve never tried to do the fighting AND the healing like that before. I think it was a little much. Plus I have to remember I’m not 22 anymore either. Never though I’d notice it, but the closer 30 gets, the more I realize my limits. Anyway, I left some stuff out about last night.

            After I got Rose back to her room and started cutting the charred remains of her clothes away from the burned skin, she stopped me. And I think this is part of what set me off so much. It wasn’t from the pain, she wanted to let me know the Faceless Bride (Marriage Spirit) was riding Nightshade after we finished with the Rose Bride. She knew I wasn’t paying attention to friendly units at the time, so she thought I should know. During all that pain and suffering…all the agony of having me touching her, she was worried about me and Nightshade. Tavia started moving her stuff in to make a palette in the floor. I used some of Snowflake’s vodka to clean Rose’s joints. I know what losing mobility can do to you and I was nothing special. As she was an Olympic swimmer, I wasn’t taking any chances with Rose. Rose probably didn’t notice but I hated myself for what I was doing. It was clean and it would help, but it was the most painful thing I could have done and still been helpful. Rose let herself fall unconscious instead of dealing with the pain. I’m pretty sure some part of me died right then. I’m not sure if the Dog will be able to bring it back.

            I sent Tavia to find Nightshade after I finished torturing Rose. Some part of my mind that still functioned realized she needed those gashes in her arm looked out. Fortunately she was already happily into the Cognac so my holistic appropriate was that much easier. She’ll be fine now. Probably won’t even have a mark to show for it. I stayed in and around Roses’s room after that to keep check on my patient. Nightshade must have sensed something was wrong with me. She came in to check on me several times throughout the night. That helped dull the blade of loathing I was feeling as I watched Rose’s scorched body sleep. I refused myself the reward of sleep as I didn’t feel I had earned any such respite.

            Around 0700 Hours, Nightshade gathered those of us that were uninjured together downstairs. Ronin had already gone for her morning run, I don’t think the others had slept either. Nightshade was trying to get an idea of how everyone was and what the next step would be. Someone asked if we could do anything to avoid this in the future and I remember telling Snowflake that we needed a fully capable surgical room. He said we’d put it on the first floor. I then stated my intention to find Our Lady’s Dead Dominion, go in, kill the bitch, and take her shit. I need to not talk when I’m sleep deprived. I think if they would have let me, I might have tried to go by myself today without sleep or help. Thankfully no one was letting me out of the House. I have some vague memory of Tavia talking about a mushroom garden, but I might have dreamed that too. Anyway, Ronin and Nightshade made me go to bed while they when to Mass and sleep respectively. Snowflake was going to go out for the day and Cupcake wanted to help Chandra learn to drive. I didn’t really see much of a need for me to be around so I agreed to sleep.

            Now it’s time for me to go check on my patient again.


            Doc’s Log: 1025 Hours, Monday, June 7, 2010

            The rest of the day yesterday was fairly quiet. After I tended to Rose, I spent some time with Nightshade learning the piano while she worked on the electric guitar.

            Mediation this morning took a while, but as I didn’t go out on patrol or train with Sebastian, I was still up pretty early. I went upstairs to tend to Rose by around 0730 Hours, and then headed off to work. I started doodling after I get everything open and I’ve been reworking the same idea since. I think it’s animal skulls but I’m not a great artist and this is just something that’s stuck in my mind. I guess I’ll keep working on it until I can figure it out.


            Doc’s Log: 1345 Hours, Monday, June 7, 2010

            By the time Nightshade stopped by with her picnic basket, I had several pages of drawings in front of me. Apparently I’m drawing skulls; wolf and bear skulls to be specific (according to the power of the Internet). I guess I could draw better this might make sense. I’m just not sure where it’s coming from, but I know I have to get it right. I stopped while we ate as I needed a break and Nightshade is always welcome company. We talked a little about the Faceless Bride, though I could tell there about a million other things she’d rather talk about. So we went back to idle chatter and other much lighter topics.

            Now she’s gone so it’s back to work, overall and on this image I’ve got stuck in my head.


            Doc’s Log: 1920 Hours, Monday, June 7, 2010

            When I got home, I was immediately pulled into a Jam Session. Nightshade had all of us she could convince to play something. It was fun, but I noticed that Karen has started calling Nightshade by her real name in front of people now. I think there should be a conversation about this, but that wasn’t the time or the place for that. Levity was the order of the moment so I decided not to be the buzzkill.

            Doc’s Log: 1100 Hours, Tuesday, June 8, 2010

            I went out to train with Sebastian again last night. I feel like there was some real progress in our session. This morning I got up a little more like normal at 0800 hours. My meditation went surprisingly quickly this morning; so much so that I was able to have breakfast with the Krewe before leaving for work. Rose was up and around, which was wonderful that she could but a terrible idea overall. I made her promise not to do anything beside hang out around the House. I didn’t put it past her to push herself back into her regular routine.

            I’ve apparently developed a new routine myself. As soon as I had the shop ready to open, I started on my drawing again. I’ve figured out now that it’s a wolf skull and bear skull, facing away from each other. There’s another, more human skull stacked on top of them. There’s already something familiar about the whole thing now but I’ll have to keep working on it to figure out what.


            Doc’s Log: 0245 Hours, Wednesday, June 9, 2010

            NIghtsahd for lunch?

            At dinner, Rose told about a new player in the Big Picture TM. She apparently talked with Mongler at some point, though I’m hoping she called him and didn’t go out looking like an arson victim. The Changelings have something called Gentry. These Gentry are kind of like what the old stories talked about in Fairy Tales. Like the real ones the Brothers Grim talked about. There was even a comparison to being like a Kerberoi within its own Dead Dominion. Anyway, these Gentry get the need to go body snatching, so they grab some pocket lint and a toothpick, cast magic into it and turn it into a Fetch. Fetches are a complete clone including memories of the people the Gentry snatch away through some kind of magic bush. The way Rose talked, Mongler is really afraid of these things. That can’t be good.

            After dinner I worked with Rose some more and I think she’s starting to respond very well to the holistic therapies. I know how spastic I was on Sunday, but she’s gotten healthier everyday. Her will is strong. She will be whole again. I’ll see to that.

            Once that was done there, was Game Night.

            I slipped out just after 0Dark00, and headed for the studio. Sebastian was there and waiting. We cut a little short tonight as I think he needed a break. I’m more confident in what I can do now, so it’s less of an issue. Don’t think I’ll let him off that easy the rest of the week though.

            Now I think it’s time for another few sketches before sleep.


            Doc’s Dream

            Anger. *sound of fists impacting meat*

            The red haze of wrath teased just outside the edges of Doc’s vision as
            he drove down the moonless road. Glancing down, the sleek Harley
            V-Rod rumbled smoothly beneath him. Doc smiled to himself as he
            looked back to the road. The paint job was truly custom as he helped
            craft it himself. The shiny body gained its dark coloring from the
            blackened flesh of a frostbite victim, while the single crimson
            styling line on either side came from a mixture of his own and
            vampiric blood. Curiously, it always glistened as though it was fresh
            and running along the length of the machine. On either side of the
            tank, just above the chrome engine and pipes sat an emblem Doc
            originally drew himself before having it drafted into a proper
            ornament. A wolf and bear skull facing opposite directions, a fanged
            human skull on top, facing out. The Darkening of the Light hexagram
            used for the Krewe symbol burned into the forehead.

            Rage. *sound of wooden chairs smashing*

            The most stalwart of Doc’s Krewe lay charred in her room within Dark
            Haven Bluffs. His medical abilities could only do so much to help her
            heal and even less to ease her agony. Jasmine’s pain came from the
            Geist known as the Rose Bride, who had been locked away in the
            DeCastro Djinn Chest for these and countless other sins. However, her
            underling still remained free. And it was this ghost with which Doc
            wanted a word.

            Easing the bike onto its stand, Doc walked into Gleason’s Irish Pub,
            the thud of his boots drawing attention as he entered. He pulled a
            bill from his pocket, flipped it on the bar, and ordered without
            taking a stool.

            “Irish Whiskey. The bottle.”

            The bartender eyed him cautiously for a moment before moving to take
            down the bottle. Doc took it without a word and headed for the
            staircase at the far end of the bar. When the bartender realized what
            he intended to do, he called out that the office was off limits. Doc
            stopped without turning and replied, “I’ve got a meeting with Big
            Sean.” The bartender’s eyes widen and Doc continued up the stairs.

            It was late (or early depending on your point of view) so the office
            was deserted, at least to mortal eyes. For Doc, a well muscled man
            sat at a card table at the back of the room, waiting nervously. His
            dingy, striped shirt hung unbuttoned at the neck and the odd trousers
            dated him. Wrapping his Stigmata Shroud around himself, Doc walked
            forward phasing from the office directly into Twilight. Big Sean
            Gleason jumped in his chair as the leather-clad Sin-Eater came over
            and sat down at the table with him.

            Setting the whiskey down on the table, Doc gave Sean a hard stare
            before finally speaking.

            “We need to talk.”

            Sean, suddenly not feeling so Big, blinked for a moment before trying
            to regain himself. “Who do ya think ya are, walkin’ inta my place
            like this?”

            Doc smiled, opened the bottle, and took a pull from it before sliding
            it back toward the ghost. Not seeing a reason to pass up a good
            whiskey, Gleason pulled from the bottle as well.

            “Let’s not worry about who I am for now, Sean. Can I call you Sean?
            Let’s focus on who you are for the moment.”

            The ghost bristled, feeling the liquor burn down his throat. “I’m Big
            Sean Gleason an’ I’ve killed men fer le...ugh!” He stopped short as
            Doc hurled the table aside, grabbed him by the throat, and slammed the
            ghost against the wall in one fluid motion. An edge hung under Doc’s
            voice that rarely surfaced as he spoke next.

            “You are a coward, a bully, ….an insect to be exterminated if you
            don’t shut up and listen to me very closely!” Sean’s feet dangled
            several inches off the ground as he made odd gurgling noises.

            Doc continued, his ire creeping further into his darkening voice.
            “It’s because of you that we found out about the Rose Bride. It’s
            because of you that Stanton Marshall and Bonnie Lynn Baker are still
            around. And it’s damn well because of you that Jasmine is smoldering
            like a piece of charcoal right now.” His grip on Sean’s throat tightened
            to such a degree that a living man’s windpipe would collapse. Sean’s
            eyes bulged and he clutched at Doc’s arm in a panic.

            With a look of pure rage on his face, Doc screamed into the ghost’s
            face. “So now here’s what’s going to happen little man. My Krewe is
            going to come see you in a few days. When you see us next, it will be
            to send you on your way. Do you understand what that means?” He
            paused and watched as the man struggled to nod. “Then you better hope
            and pray to your Catholic God that you’re ready. Because if you’re
            not, I promise you what we did to the Rose Bride will seem like a
            picnic in Donegal compared to what I’m going to do to you!”

            With that, Doc swung Gleason down flat on the office floor, picked up
            what was left of the bottle of whiskey, and walked out.

            ***

            Doc woke up late Sunday afternoon with an odd grin. His head was a
            little more clear after getting some rest and his anger had oddly
            seemed to pass. He felt like he’d been dreaming, but couldn’t
            remember exactly what. Doc figured he should go check on Jasmine and
            the rest of his Krewe. Now if he could just figure out how to explain
            away his previous outburst that morning about the alchemical katanas


            Jasmine’s Diary

            Tuesday, June 8, 2010, Morning

            I’m still convalescing, and I’m still in pain, but largely thanks to Doc’s amazing abilities as a holistic doctor, I’m much better now than I was.

            I was so intent on recording the events of the fight against the Rose Bride when I wrote yesterday, and it was so painful to do so, that I did not really write about the aftermath. When we returned home, Doc immediately saw to my wounds. He seemed somewhat embarrassed to have to cut my burned clothes from me in order to tend to my wounds, but I wasn’t bothered by it. I was simply in a lot of pain, and he was doing what he had to in order to help me.

            But I think Doc was more angry than embarrassed. He doesn’t like it when we’re hurt, and I think he felt personally responsible for my wounds, as though he might have been able to protect me better.

            So I told him that he shouldn’t feel responsible. I did what I did and suffered the Rose Bride’s onslaught to protect everyone else. I knew that I would be horribly hurt or killed, but I also knew I was the only one who could keep her from escaping, and I was willing to face death to see her imprisoned. He shouldn’t be angry with himself for allowing me to do what he would have done in my shoes.

            But there is often a gap between what we know rationally and what we feel. And in Doc’s case, the trauma of his experiences in Afghanistan still weigh heavily on him. So I cannot blame him for remaining angry for the next day or so while he worked through his frustration.

            That said, he was always very gentle and caring when he tended to my wounds. I was impressed by his skills as a medic, and glad to have him looking after me.

            Unfortunately, he was not happy when I told him that I’d seem the marriage spirit whispering to both Natalie and Karen, pushing them toward a marriage for which I don’t think they are prepared. I worry about them, and knew that Doc would too. I hoped that by telling him, he could inform Natalie of what I saw so that she can make a wiser decision about Karen right now…if she’s even capable of making wise decisions when it comes to things like this.

            Tavia has also done a fine job tending to me. She doesn’t know much about medicine, but she’s been at my bedside almost constantly to help in any way she can. The fruit she brings me has been a true blessing.

            I called Jean on Sunday afternoon. I had to fight through the pain to talk to him for long, but I cancelled our training sessions for the next week. He was quite worried, and I admitted that I had burned myself badly. I think he could sense that it was worse than I was letting on, but he seemed content to leave me be and not ask too many questions.

            If it had been Richard, he would have shown up at my door, worried sick and ready to do whatever he could to help me. I’ve been missing him a great deal over these past few days.

            I spent most of the past two days sleeping. Well, not really sleeping, as sleeping is difficult with my burns, but meditating, deeply and completely. I have allowed myself to sink deep into the recesses of my own psyche with the Drowned Bride. We’ve sought solace and comfort in the deep, cool, soothing waters of the ocean depths. We’ve drifted peacefully there in that lightless bastion of my soul, detached from my suffering. It has been rejuvenating, and I feel that it has helped me recover even more quickly than Doc expected.

            In fact, I was able to attend breakfast this morning, though everyone looked at me as though I was insane for getting out of bed in my state. I think it was good to get up and move about a bit, though I can’t say I was comfortable. Still, by tomorrow, I think I’ll be much better off.

            Over breakfast, I congratulated everyone for a job well done in the battle. I told them how glad I was that they remained safe. I also complimented Simon specifically, for if he had not completed the ritual so speedily, then I may very well have been killed by the Rose Bride.

            Also during breakfast, I was told that Gwen had an encounter with Marjorie’s ghost while she was out jogging Sunday morning. Marjorie was confused and trying to find her way home, of course. Gwen didn’t want to tell her she was dead without easing her into it, but Marjorie was not willing to come to Dark Haven Bluff to chat with the rest of us, so Gwen was forced to leave her be for the time. I suppose we will have to try again later. She shouldn’t be too hard to find, as she seems to be wandering in the area of her anchor quite a bit right now.


            Tuesday, June 8, 2010, Afternoon

            As I was feeling better this afternoon, I called Mongler to find out if he knew anything that might help us with Shatina’s case. I explained what we knew to him, and he felt that her situation might be related to his people. He explained a bit to me about fetches and the “The Gentry,” and offered to put me in touch with another Changeling known as Cock Robin. He does not think I will like Cock Robin, but Robin is the leading authority in our city on fetches, and right now, I fear Shatina might be a fetch of some sort.

            I also asked Mongler how his dates with Gretchen went. He seemed a bit embarrassed to talk about it, but he also sounded like he enjoyed them, and that brightened my mood considerably. He seems to like Gretchen, and my impression is that they are hitting it off well. He did comment on her utter lack of tact, but he didn’t seem so much bothered as surprised by it, which is good. I do wish them luck.


            Pop Culture

            The Hitchhiker’s Guide to the Galaxy

            Since I’ve been bedridden, Gwen downloaded some audio books onto my iPod for me, including my beloved Keats, Jane Eyre, some Agatha Christie, and The Hitchhiker’s Guide to the Galaxy. Since she often references the The Hitchhiker’s Guide, I decided I would listen to it first.

            I must confess that it was not really to my taste. I’m not particularly inclined toward comedies in the first place, and the book struck me as completely absurd. I talked to Gwen about it and expressed my misgivings, and she helped to put things in perspective for me. Apparently the entire point of the book is to be absurd, which seems…well…absurd to me, but I can understand why people like Gwen would enjoy it.

            Honestly, Gwen and I are so different, and yet we get along with each other so well. I suppose we balance each other, like yin and yang. I’m certainly glad to have her as my friend.


            The Gentry and Fetches

            Richard explained to me a bit about the things his people, the Changelings, have to worry about, and they sound every bit as terrifying as the Kerberoi.

            The biggest enemy of the Changelings are The Gentry. The Gentry are specific individuals of god-like power who rule their own domains in another realm that’s connected to our world. They are not unlike the Kerberoi, from what I gathered. Unlike the Kerberoi, however, they like to meddle in our world, and frequently kidnap people to serve them in whatever capacity they wish. A person taken by one of the Gentry is changed by the experience, and if they escape back to our world, they return as a Changeling, like Mongler or Tommy Knocker.

            When a Gentry kidnaps someone, they sometimes leave a replica of that person in her place so that her disappearance will go unnoticed. The replica, called a fetch, is both real and illusory, fashioned from odds and ends but appearing in all ways to be a real person. Given all the recent talk on the matter, I couldn’t help but ask Mongler if a fetch was anything like a “pod person,” which is a movie reference Gwen and Tavia bring up occasionally. Much to my chagrin, he said that was a relatively accurate comparison.

            Fetches sometimes work on behalf of their Gentry creators, so if Shatina is a fetch, she might be up to no good. On the other hand, according to Mongler, some fetches don’t even known they are fetches, and think they really are the person they have replaced. From what we’ve learned so far, I think that is probably the case for Shatina, if she is a fetch. Unfortunately, Mongler says fetches can sometimes be “sleeper agents,” who seem normal until their Gentry master awakens them, so even if Shatina doesn’t have any nefarious purpose right now, she might be dangerous in the future.

            Gwen’s Journal

            June 6th

            I never got around to sleeping. Did my research and think I can manage the ritual for spectral photography now. Should be pretty interesting if I get the chance to play with it some. I went on my run a lot earlier than usual - I was just too keyed up after our run in with Rosie to settle so out I went. It must’ve been earlier than I realized because during my run I spied a woman dressed up in a familiar blue dress along the side of the road. She was looking over her shoulder like she worried about being followed or something. Oh yeah - she was a ghost, but someone who wasn’t in the know wouldn’t have picked up on that. Still, she seemed pretty nervous. Based on what I knew from Jazz, this was Marjorie, the spectral hitchhiker we’d inadvertently stirred up when she and Alexia committed their B&E at the Wilson (or was it Williams?) house.

            OK - now, picture this. A ghost, that doesn’t realize she’s dead, standing on the side of the road near one of the legendary creepy houses in New Haven. And I’m standing there in bikers and a tank top trying to convince her to come into the Bluffs so we can break her death to her gently. Who’s surprised this didn’t work? HA! I let her be, but I think Alexia or Simon’s gonna have to be the one to convince her to come in and let us work things out for her.

            Once I was back at the Bluffs I got cleaned up and ready for Mass. I wouldn’t leave until after breakfast, but figured I’d use my time as efficiently as possible. I still had some time to kill so I went online and downloaded a few audiobooks onto my ipod. I hadn’t used it lately since I got a one with more memory, but I figured it’d work to snag some things for Jazz. She would be stuck in bed and trying to recover for a bit. I figured the books would be an easy way to pass the time. Made sure she had some of the romantic and morbid stuff she was into, but also dumped a few of my favs in there too. She’d promised to read the Hitchhikers Guide, if I gave Keats a try. I haven’t told her yet but I downloaded a copy of his Complete Poems and I’ve read one or two a day over the past week. Yeah...full of symbolism and themes of love and death...it fits Jazz, it really does. Me? It’s great for helping me go to sleep. No chases, no fights, no mysteries to solve, no sex, and no comedy - what’s the point?! So not my version of entertainment. But I see why Jazz likes it. So now it’s her turn. Maybe she’ll see the genius in Douglas’s writing...or maybe she’ll at least laugh a little, that’s what I’m hoping for.

            I left the ipod on Jazz’s nightstand before going down to breakfast. Doc hasn’t been to bed yet and it shows. He went on this tangent about all of us training with katanas because they were cool and necessary in case we got into a fight. I’m down with the training but his reasoning is a bit...off. Of course his desire to head straight down into the underworld to destroy Our Lady of the Pathetic needed to be tempered a little bit too. I think if we’d said OK he would’ve headed straight there without taking time to plan or even rest up before hand, never mind the fact we’re a woman down at the moment.

            We convinced him to get some sleep after Nat suggested we take it easy for a couple of days while waiting for Jazz to recover from her injuries. None of us had a problem with that and Doc even suggested we start considering putting in a decent surgical theater to help with injuries in the future. Apparently there’s a room on the first floor with an observation room attached that Simon and Tavia thought would work nicely.

            I went off to Mass then came back finally ready to get some sleep. When I got up I did some more studying on Spectral Photography while Nat played the piano in the music room. Not really enough bass for me, but hearing the music coming from the room was nice and kind of soothing. Pretty quiet day all things considered.


            June 7

            Jazz was feeling better today and I spent some time this morning with her just talking. Nothing major but it was good to just chill. No reason to leave her totally alone - ok not totally alone, Tavia’s camped out in her room and Doc’s doc Xerxes hasn’t moved from her doorway since he got here yesterday.

            I didn’t want to tire her out so after talking for a little while I went off and started installing the new keypad locks on the doors. I’ve got these locks on all the doors in the basement - well, all the doors we’ve marked for use. There’s some places down there I haven’t been in and I’m not gonna without Simon or Tavia being right there to tell the house it’s OK for me to be there. After the skeletons in the crypt rising up at me I’ve learned a healthy respect for the spirit of the house. All in all a quiet day. We all focused on our own projects and hobbies today. I think this downtime is a good thing for all of us.


            June 8

            Jazz felt up to coming down to breakfast today. She’s moving pretty slowly and kind of gingerly but that’s no surprise. She’s recovering from some major injuries. Doc warned her to take it easy and she agreed - but being Jazz she also reminded us that we needed to deal with Big Sean before Saturday.

            I’m guessing we’ll start dealing with that tomorrow. Since I was sort of at loose ends I went on and headed over to Shatina’s house to watch the comings and goings there for a few hours. Nothing really exciting there, just regular folks moving about their business. Not really sure what I expected but I kind of felt like I wasted my time there. Over dinner Jazz said she’d talked to Richard Mongler over at the college. His kind - the changelings have their own big bads like the Kerberoi known as the Gentry. According to him these are guys you do not want interested in you. Sometimes they snatch folks that gain their interest and leave a creature (creation?) called a Fetch in that person’s place. The fetch is supposed to take the person’s place and may not even know it isn’t really the person it’s replacing. Mongler’s going to put Jazz in touch with their resident Fetch expert Cock Robin (the names these people have...wow). Seems given what little we know now there’s a good chance Shatina’s been replaced with a Fetch. So now I gotta ask - if she’s a fetch, what the hell’s happened to her brother?

            The party and the full moon are rapidly approaching so I can’t really worry about Shatina right now. I need to be ready to help out dealing with Sean and getting the security on the house as tight as I can make it before the party Saturday. With luck we’ll be able to take care of Bonnie Lynn and Stanton Marshal, and hopefully we won’t have to deal with anything coming out of Tavia’s paintings this weekend either. That would put a damper on just about everything. Suppose I should talk to Anders and see if he’s gonna come keep me company during this party...eh, I’ll worry about that later for now...sleep.


            End Session XXXIX

            That mostly covers it. Natalie’s antics mostly centered on getting various Krewe members involved in her jam sessions. Natalie was playing electric guitar (medicorely) and piano (well). Karen played electric guitar, Simon played cello, Doc played some piano (mostly what Natalie had taught) and Alexia threatened to learn drums. Natalie also looked into her 27 club some more.

            Last session also covered a few days, three days this time. Mostly was finishing tying up loose ends before the party, including dealing with Big Sean and the monthly paintings vigil. There was, however, much fun; Natalie discovered things about her daughter thanks to Karen, half the Krewe mucking into Jasmine’s love life, Gwen got Tavia utterly pissed at her, Gwen and Natalie took body shots off each other in a bar for a good cause (sorta). Join us, won’t you?

                11-22-2013, 05:42 AM
                Thanks for the kind words as always Darck Child. Omenseer I will be sure Jazzy's player sees that.

                It’s that time again folks. Time to catch up with our heroes in New Haven. You will be pleased to know Natalie is back in the game, as is of course Doc, Jasmine and Gwen. This session covers about three days again, though more events do happen then last time. We build up to the big party this time, as they take care of a bunch of stuff before that happens. And some very amusing interpersonal comments.


                Session XXXIX

                Doc’s Log

                Doc’s Log: 1100 Hours, Wednesday, June 9, 2010

                Meditation this morning went about as well as normal. I was able to come down for breakfast with everyone, which is starting to be more common these days. Nightshade was actually awake (or still up, I’m not sure which). She told us that her daughter has started to see dead people in mirrors. That was how it started for Nightshade as a little girl too. Apparently Karen told her about getting in contact and thankfully this went well. Now Nightshade is thinking about going to see her. She’s confused and concerned so she asked the whole Krewe for their thoughts. I didn’t say anything at the time but I almost fell out of my chair when she invited everyone into her real life like that. Rose tried to be supportive, though if you didn’t know these two, it would have sounded really bad. Nightshade took it as Rose intended though and everything was smooth...

                Right up until something about Rose’s future Olympian came up. I’m not one to use stereotypes for much given my relationship with Nightshade and currently I live with eight women. So when I say that suddenly the Man-hating Monster hit, well I don’t know if there is a best of ways to mean for that, but still. Ronin went OFF. Apparently because the guy from the Ivory Coast didn’t go out of his way to stalk Rose (after she told him not to come by remember) after her “accident” so that he could shower her with flowers meant he was useless and completely not worth wasting any more time on romantically or otherwise. This came about when Rose mentioned innocently that she was a little disappointed that Jean hadn’t called her. That I will admit is grounds for losing some points, but Ronin’s litany of condemnation sounded like something out of an over the top teen drama. I will take this moment to point out that Cupcake was kinda staring with her mouth open just a little bit, Nightshade wasn’t saying anything and looking at her plate, Chandra looked like she wanted some popcorn, Tavia was confused, and Gretchen…was probably licking dust off the shelves in the Library.

                Cupcake slipped off to the phone booth in the hall. The other ladies dispersed as normal. Snowflake looked at me as though afraid to reengage the subject before asking what just happened. I couldn’t help but be almost as bad as Ronin by pointing out that sometimes there are too many women around and insanity creeps in…I think it was something about estrogen evaporating into the local atmosphere or something. He asked me what we should do. I said that normally we’d stay out of things like this, but because everyone went batshit, we were going to have to find a way to help this boy. Then I overheard Cupcake talking to what I could only assume was Jean, so we smiled to each other and moved that way.

                Then things went from bad (in my opinion) to worse when Ronin offhandedly commented that “just because you’re a foreigner, doesn’t mean you’re excused from knowing how things work.” As Chandra was standing right there, she couldn’t have missed it. Given the look on her suddenly more ashen face, I don’t think she misunderstood any part of that little diatribe. Tavis tried to help by mentioning that Rose seemed to get along very well with Karl. Before the Indian vampire that lives with us could do anything, Ronin swallowed her foot further by blurting out that Karl was “creepy looking.” Every ounce of Tavia took on a menacing quality and she demanded that Ronin recant her verbal assault on her cousin. Snowflake and I were very notably preparing for the worse as Tavia had that scary look in her eye and Ronin was so wrapped up in her own little world that she really didn’t know or care that she’d said anything wrong. After an uninspired apology from Ronin and a brief talking down from Snowflake and I, the girls went their separate ways. I was never so glad to see Ronin go to the office. We learned that Cupcake had informed Jean where he could send flowers and made sure he knew not to mention anything about getting help. Satisfied, I headed to work. I’m still not sure Snowflake fully grasped what happened, but he went to check on Tavia in the mannequin room. After that, a day at work seemed soothing. At least there I was assured of no attempted fratricide.


                Doc’s Log: 1525 Hours, Wednesday, June 9, 2010

                Nightshade came over with her picnic basket for lunch and we talked more about her daughter. Apparently Karen and I are both going when the trip actually happens. I’m not sure how that’s going to go, but I’m willing to be there for support nonetheless. I tried to delicately ask how she was going to explain having us both as lovers but also the closeness in age between Karen and her daughter. I had thought about that topic a lot but I still hadn’t found a way to put it that didn’t make it sound dirty. Nightshade took it in stride though. If anything, I think she more suspected I was trying to talk her out of taking Karen instead of the weirdness with presenting her daughter to a lover that was only three years older. After food and serious discussions, Nightshade had me brush her hair again. I think that I needed that kind of innocent intimacy after the morning and my concerns for her daughter. After she left, I started working on my sketch again.

                I think I’ve decided that I saw this image in a dream. That’s both cool and creepy given that it’s apparently three skulls. But it seems to me the more I draw it and the closer it looks to what I have in my head, that this is some kind of emblem or identifying design. It just feels important.


                Doc’s Log: 0425 Hours, Thursday, June 10, 2010

                We had Taiwanese tonight so Rose was very happy. Over dinner, we discussed that we had a very short time left before the party. This left Big Sean as an obstacle to getting Stanton Marshall to pass on. The love story over a hundred years in the making could not be ruined now as Rose could not allow it. And I have to admit, I gave my word to help with this in exchange for Marshall’s rifle. It was only fair.

                We decided to go later tonight (Thursday) armed with ceremonies, a willingness to talk to him (after bleeding on him so he made sense if needs be), and whatever other means necessary. Cupcake planned to go with her redhead outfit, probably just for fun or Chandra even if it wasn’t necessary anymore. No one could really think of a reason to tell her not too. After we had all those plans in place, I took Rose back upstairs for her treatment.

                She is truly responding at a miraculous rate even for a Sin-Eater. She swears that I’m just that good of a healer. I don’t doubt my skill, but I’m still very surprised that she’s healed so much. I think I might be able to completely heal her tomorrow if she is still responding this well by the time we need to prepare. Once that was done, I slipped out a little earlier than usual and headed to go train with Sebastian. Now I’m going to take a shower and crash.


                Doc’s Log: 1400 Hours, Thursday, June 10, 2010

                Been a good day so far. Meditation came and went without much fuss. Didn’t get up quite as early today though. Breakfast was me grabbing a little food and waving bye before running to work. Other than stocking and helping people as they came in, I was back to my obsessive drawing.

                Lunch was nice and calm. No heavy discussions this time, just happy small talk.

                Nightshade sent me a text that flowers arrived for Rose. I hope the poor guy didn’t actually show up. Who knows what might have happened. Now I’m back to drawing. Tonight we get to give an Irishman his last night on Earth speech. And the intention is for no one to get screwed. We’ll see how this goes.


                Doc’s Log: 2015 Hours, Thursday, June 10, 2010

                Cupcake kept dinner light tonight. But how weird is it that Cupcake burned her hand today? I was able to fix her up pretty easily but I just wasn’t expecting wounds from non-combat. I think I’ve been a medic too long. We’ll need to be agile in case Big Sean isn’t willing to cooperate. Rose had nearly recovered by the time I came up to give her a check up tonight. I decided that it would be wise to have her at full strength when we deal with Sean, so I gathered up my goodies and ceremonially healed her. Surprisingly it took less than five minutes and didn’t wipe out all my Plasm. That’s useful as well. We’re still not sure that Big Sean is going to “go quietly into that good night.”

                Now it’s time to head out. Gleason’s Irish Pub, here we come.


                Doc’s Log: 0350 Hours, Friday, June 11, 2010

                Sebastian worked me over again tonight. All the details had to be at least as good as he does it. I have cramps in places I forgot I had. But that’s not the important part right now. Later it will be though.

                We got to Gleason’s Irish Pub as a group while the place was in full swing. I brought Marshall’s rifle but left it in the car for obvious reasons. We knew going in that taking something personal of Sean’s and then getting up to the office with it was going to take a distraction. Ronin said that she and Nightshade could just do some body shots. I think it was originally meant as a joke. Then Nightshade immediately jumped on that idea. Ronin looked up at me as if asking permission. First of all, I’m not worried about a straight woman flirting with Nightshade. Second, Nightshade has enough going on with two lovers. Third, Anders is definitely not a passing thing for Ronin. So I didn’t see where this was a terrible thing. I also wasn’t going anywhere until it was over. Rose, of course marched upstairs immediately, wanting nothing to do with such a display. I do not know a straighter woman.

                But the distraction worked…very well. Snowflake and I took a few extra seconds before we remembered we had to go upstairs. Cupcake nabbed the trinket and we were set. No one really tried to stop us so we all headed up. Rose had already started talking to Sean about moving on when we got there. He agreed that maybe it was time. I know that ghosts are as different as people and I’ve never really seen him before, but he just looked tired to me. So Ronin and Rose performed the ceremony to help him Pass On. It sounded a lot like Ronin’s version was a stylized reworking of Last Rites. I thought that was very fitting all the way around. I poured Sean a drink to help send him off and then he just faded away.

                We came prepared for anything from refusal to attack. But really Sean was just happy to finally rest. I know it’s our job, but I don’t have the words to describe how good it feels to put things right in the world like that.

                On the way back to the House, Ronin and Rose were both being mopey about not having dates to the party. Ronin climbed up on the diving board over her personal pool of crazy about Jean again until I asked if she was talking about the guy that sent flowers addressed to Rose at the House today? Ronin stopped short and looked around. Cupcake caught her attention and looked at Ronin like she might do something very unpleasant if she asked the obvious question in front of Rose. Finally taking a hint, Ronin changed gears and just made small talk as she watched how happy Rose actually seemed. I like Ronin but sometimes she’s as thick as a tree trunk. Fortunately Cupcake seemed to find a knothole this time. Once we made it back to the House, we all congregated in the new bar Snowflake finished up earlier in the day. Nightshade was already working on the Cognac so I offered to mix drinks for the others. It’s been a while since I mixed for my unit, but I don’t think I did too bad.

                After everyone started to shuffle off to their rooms, the Library, or wherever, I headed out to get my final dance lesson before the party. I was nervous and excited. Like I mentioned, Sebastian didn’t disappoint, though I’m not sure he didn’t dislocate. But I know I can do this now. I owe Sebastian for this. I don’t think he knows just how much this means to me. Though now I have to sleep. We have to be ready to fight with a painting or two tonight.


                Doc’s Log: 1015 Hours, Friday, June 11, 2010

                Breakfast was more like normal today. No Nightshade in the AM and mostly people just enjoying Cupcake’s labors. Then I headed off to work.

                So far, it’s been a usual day. Not horribly busy, helping the people that come by on the way to or from work, and sketching. The skull on top is apparently a vampire’s skull as it has fangs, but I’ve noticed something else becoming clearer to me now too.

                Our Krewe symbol is on the skull’s forehead.

                Wolf. Bear. Vampire. All predators, all mostly hunting at night. There’s some metaphor in there, but I’m not comfortable enough with anything to be definitive yet.


                Doc’s Log: 1850 Hours, Friday, June 11, 2010

                I closed up the shop as fast as I could tonight so I could get back to Dark Haven Bluffs ASAP. I wanted to be sure I could help with the preparations. I know that staying outside the ritual room is actually helpful but I don’t ever feel like I’m pulling my weight. I realized just as I got out of the cab that I have got to start remembering to make the Elixir of Alertness every month so we have it on hand.

                Snowflake apparently had a feeling about tonight. After dinner, he pulled me to the side and gave me crash course on the binding ritual. He said he wanted to make sure I knew what it sounded like if something went wrong. I’m not sure the power of my blood would allow it to work, but I’ll be committing that one to memory. In the here and now, I knew it was a very helpful precaution in case something happened, I could help the rest of the Krewe.

                There isn’t much else to say right now. We’re just setting up and waiting. I’m going to go clean and load the shotgun and try to keep centered. I always get wound up so tight during these vigils.


                Doc’s Log: 0245 Hours, Saturday, June 12, 2010

                Looks like we win again. For another month at least. It got a little scary for a bit.

                The chanting took on an edge of fear, there were other nervous sounds, and then everything went quiet. I was getting anxious but I didn’t hear the binding ritual, so I stayed the hell out. Eventually Ronin, Cupcake, Tavia, and Snowflake shuffled out. Tavia and Snowflake definitely looked exhausted. We asked what happened and damn I really wish we hadn’t. Apparently the Hanger in the Void managed to stretch a claw through into our world, but they managed to force him back just hard enough to seal the portrait again.

                Rose was very unhappy that things got close and is back to finding a way to permanently exercise these paintings. We’ve already covered why that’s asking for more trouble, but this was not the time to revisit that again. The party is tonight and we all need to sleep.


                Jasmine’s Diary

                Wednesday, June 9, 2010, Morning

                I’m doing better every day. Today, I’m still in pain, but I’m feeling a bit more like my old self again.

                Breakfast this morning was particularly chatty. I mentioned my disappointment that Jean had not so much as called to check on me since I cancelled our training sessions and told him I’d been badly injured. Gwen’s response to this was quite animated, and she told me that if Jean wasn’t even making the effort, then he was not worth my time. The Drowned Bride is quite disappointed in him as well. He hasn’t lived up to her romantic ideals.

                I personally think Gwen and the Bride are being too harsh, but then again, perhaps they are right. I don’t really know how I feel about Jean, and he has not been very aggressive in following up on our date almost two weeks ago, so maybe he doesn’t know how he feels about me either. I cannot really blame him, as I have been rather cold. I can’t help it. Or maybe he just doesn’t know how to proceed.

                Regardless, a simple phone call to check on me would have been nice. I’ve been a bit depressed about it.

                Natalie surprised us all by being at breakfast this morning, and it soon became apparent why she was there. She is worried about her daughter, who is finding out more and more about Natalie every week, it seems. Her daughter also claims to be seeing visions of dead people like Natalie did before she became a sin-eater. We all have some experience like that. I used to see premonitions of people dying. It was one such premonition that indirectly led to my own death. So I think we can all identify with the difficulty of adapting to such visions.

                Natalie wants to reconnect with her daughter, and if her daughter really is having these visions (and not just pretending to be like her mother, whom she rather questionably views as a role model), then I think now is a good time for Natalie to seek out her daughter and try to help her. She has to accept responsibility for her daughter sooner or later, and quite frankly, she’s been running from it for too long. Yes, I suppose one could argue that her daughter is better off without Natalie leading her astray, but since she seems obsessed with her mother anyway, Natalie needs be there for her. I told Natalie as much after breakfast. I do hope she takes my words to heart instead of dismissing them like she does many things I say.

                On a more comforting note, I also asked Natalie about Karen and was assured that they were not getting married any time soon. I’m relieved to see that Natalie recognizes that neither she nor Karen is ready for something like that. I did try to encourage her, though, because she seemed a bit down, what with everything else weighing on her mind. I don’t know that I succeeded, but I did try. I may not like Natalie very much but that doesn’t mean I can’t care for her well-being, and she does seem to be taking more responsibility lately.


                Wednesday, June 9, 2010, Night

                I spent some time out in the garden with Alexia and Chandra today. I didn’t help them with their work, of course. I’m still in bad shape from my burns and Doc has already chided me several times about not staying in bed to rest. But it was nice to get out a bit, even if the sun was a bit unpleasant on my bandaged skin.


                Thursday, June 10, 2010, Afternoon

                I’m feeling much better today. In fact, I’ve healed to the point that Doc will be able to perform a healing ceremony on me to mend the rest of my burns. The only reason he did not do so before was because we were concerned that he might need the spiritual plasm for other things if something dire came up, but fortunately we seem to be safe for the time being, and with the party in a few nights, we all need to be in tip-top shape. Also, more plasm has gathered in our haunts, so we should be able to refill our spiritual reserves a bit.

                This evening, we are going to try and help Sean Gleason’s ghost pass on, which shouldn’t be too difficult now that the Rose Bride has been removed from the picture. And then, tomorrow night, we will need to make sure the Outsiders don’t escape from Tavia’s paintings, which is always a threat during the night of a new moon. And then Saturday we are holding Bonnie-Lynn’s party! As we are going to be so busy these next few days, I’m glad Doc will be able to make me fully healthy again.


                Thursday, June 10, 2010, Late Night

                I’m feeling very happy this evening. Yes, happy. It’s not often I can say that, is it? It has been a very good day.

                First off, I received flowers and a get well card from Jean today! I don’t know how he found my address, but receiving those from him really perked up my spirits. I called him and he apologized for the delay, saying that it took him some time to track me down. I was quite heartwarming to know that he cares, and it made me very much miss our training sessions.

                Then, when Doc returned, he checked out my burns and decided that I’d recovered enough that he could heal me the rest of the way with a ceremony, so we spent some time on that, and once we were done, I felt as good as new. No burns or other lingering injuries. He really can work miracles!

                Then we went to Sean Gleason’s bar to help him pass on. Since it was busy, this meant we had to pull off a little trickery. I snuck upstairs to speak with Sean and convince him that it was time to let go, while Alexia tried to filch one of the memorabilia items near the bar so that we could use it for a ceremony to help Sean move on. She needed a distraction to help her steal the item without anyone noticing, though, and I was shocked when Gwen suggested doing “body shots” with Natalie. I didn’t really know what body shots were, but I found out, and decided I would hurry upstairs before I saw more. I would expect such behavior from Natalie, but I’m quite frankly mortified that Gwen was up for it!

                In any event, once I explained that the Rose Bride was gone and Stanton Marshall was dead, Sean Gleason’s tired ghost seemed ready and willing to move to rejoin the karmic cycle. All he needed was a final push, and so Gwen and I performed a ritual to help him. Gwen took the lead since she and Sean are both Catholic, and I think it was very fulfilling for her to help him find peace. I don’t really know exactly how the sin and forgiveness dynamic of Catholicism works, as it seems somewhat formulaic to me, but I do wonder if helping Sean pass on makes up for the body shots.

                Then we returned home with a Job well done, and we decided to celebrate together in the brand new bar room, which Simon just finished renovating. He is very industrious! I don’t drink, of course, but being there with the rest of the krewe, with everyone in such good spirits was almost intoxicating by itself! I was healed, and for once we all felt very close to one another, not as dysfunctional as usual. We really felt like a family, and I was reminded that despite our little disagreements, the krewe is my family now, and this is where I belong.

                I’ve been listening to the B-52s since I returned to my room, a bit too keyed up to sleep. I am comfortable floating in the depths of my melancholia, but it’s nice to break the surface and feel the sun from time to time.


                Saturday, June 12, 2010, Very Early Morning

                I’m exhausted, and need to sleep. We have been up all night making sure Tavia’s paintings don’t spawn horrors from the Abyss. Fortunately, all is well, though according to Simon, one of the creatures managed to get a claw through the frame before he was able to force it back. That worries me. Simon assures me it was a fluke. But all it takes is once such “fluke” to allow catastrophe to descend on us all. I’d like to seek a more permanent solution to sealing the paintings.

                Otherwise, yesterday was uneventful. I would have written entries earlier, but we were all so busy making preparations for Bonnie-Lynn’s ball (which is tonight) that I couldn’t really find a spare moment. I spent much of the day helping Bonnie-Lynn prepare. She’s beside herself with excitement, and her excitement is infectious. I hope they can find their “happily ever after” and pass on peacefully.

                Now, I’m off to bed.

                Footnotes

                Death’s Visions
                The members of the krewe all had some connection to death even before becoming sin-eaters. I saw premonitions of people dying, and they always came true. When I was a child, I tried to warn people when I saw such visions, but the other people in our neighborhood thought I was cursed and brought ill-fortune on them. My parents believed in me, but it was still difficult growing up because everyone who lived around us feared me. It’s one reason I didn’t have any friends growing up. Though I suppose that made it much easier for me to devote myself to my swimming.

                Gwen says that she had the ability to know how people died by examining the corpse. It’s one thing that made her such a good detective. She’s lucky; her “gift” seemed more a boon than a curse.

                I don’t know exactly what Simon or Doc or Alexia’s experiences were before their death. But Natalie seems to have had the worst of it. She saw visions of dead people in mirrors. That itself is bad enough, though no more traumatic than my own premonitions. What made her experience so bad was her parents, who thought she was demon possessed and put her through some very torturous experiences in an attempt to save her. Instead of being understanding, like my parents were, they were the ones who through their daughter cursed.

                So, if Natalie’s daughter really is going through the same thing, Natalie should try to help her. On the other hand, maybe her parents have learned from their mistakes with Natalie. That would be nice, if a bit optimistic.

                Gwen's Journal

                June 9 (Wednesday)

                Well. I managed to stick my foot in it this morning. Like normal I went for a run and came home to find Nat joining everyone at breakfast. This is not normal y’know? She’s usually the sleep till noon type. But breakfast is a great time to catch us all together and I guess that’s what she was wanting. She told us that Karen had been in contact with her daughter and through their conversations learned that her girl also had the same type of death visions that Nat did before she became a geist.

                `What do you say to that huh? I mean seriously? Every one of us had some affinity with death beforehand I think. Me? According to my journal, I could always tell exactly how a murder went down. No wonder I was a good detective – the ghosts themselves helped me out. But I’m guessing everyone else was thinking the same thing as me. Nat needs to talk to her daughter. She needs to connect and make sure this kid has some help dealing with the visions. From the sounds of things her grandparents won’t be all that understanding about it, so Nat needs to step up. But of course none of us said that. We did say we’d support her if she tried to get in touch though, so hopefully she’ll accept the offer for what it is.

                After that is when I apparently started pissing people off. Jazz mentioned that she’d canceled her training sessions with Jean – as she should’ve – and I asked how things were with him. She said he hadn’t contacted her since she canceled even though she told him she was hurt. I was kind of surprised by this you know? He was interested enough that Jazz flat out noticed and even stepped out of her comfort zone and went out with him and Dude didn’t call her? Didn’t send her flowers? What the hell man?! Jazz tried to point out that he didn’t know where she lived but you know – that’s not an excuse. I’m not saying he should’ve shown up on our doorstep, but how hard would it be to call her office and get the address for a delivery guy? He’s one of her students – the department’s got a damn secretary right?

                Nat popped off with something about how that’d be a bit stalkerish if he went hunting down her address. Doc looked at me like I was crazy, and Alexia seemed to think about it a bit hard. Simon just looked confused.

                Now look, I know the law. I get it. But how is it stalkerish if a guy you haven’t told to step off, that you’ve spent time with almost daily, tries to find a way to get in touch with you when you’re injured? Hell, he could’ve sent flowers to her office. The dept. sec. could’ve called to tell her they were there. I would’ve gone and gotten them for her. I’m not saying the dude should’ve hired a singing telegram or something – but a small arrangement, or a card…heck even a balloon – just something to let her know she wasn’t forgotten. That he was thinking of her. That he cared. Dude lost serious points, but for some reason I get the feeling that the others think I’m being unreasonable for thinking this way.

                I just want my friend to be happy. Being ignored is not the way that’s gonna happen so damn right I’m irked on her behalf. Dude should’ve put forth some freakin’ effort.

                I took off to try and work off a little of the irritation but a bit later I heard Alexia talking with Chandra about helping Jean out. I cornered her wanting to know why she’d do something like that. If the dude didn’t show the initiative on his own he doesn’t deserve any help from us. She said he wasn’t from here so maybe he didn’t get our customs.

                …

                OK so she had a point. But I wasn’t ready to cut him some slack and pretty much said so…I might’ve said something about being foreign was no excuse but I don’t know I was too focused on my righteous anger to pay attention. Yeah – if you’re getting up on a soap box about something you usually don’t give two flips about then you’ve already past the point of reason I think.

                About then Tavia mentioned that Jazz also liked Carl – as in Cousin Carl – and thought he’d be quite attentive (or something like that – my brain sort of broke at the thought of Jazz with Creepy Carl).
                This is where I really stuck my foot in it. I actually called him Creepy. I think I managed to stop myself from saying anything about his funky squished looking head – but I definitely called him Creepy and that not only pissed Tavia off but it upset her. And at the time I wasn’t thinking so I couldn’t see what the big deal was. Yeah, Carl’s look is kind of creepy but he’s a nice guy. Saying that didn’t help though. I’d insulted him and Tavia was not pleased by that at all. I finally left rather than continue to dig a hole when I wasn’t really sure what the problem was. Sitting in my office and going over it in my head a few times I realized what I’d done.

                I insulted her family. Tavia’s always talked about Carl very fondly, and he seemed pretty close to her when he visited. If someone insulted my family I’d probably pound them. Jamie and I haven’t spoken since before I was shot, but I’d still have to hurt someone for screwing with my family. Actually, I kind of feel that way about the krewe – so I got why she was so pissed at me. When I got home I found her and apologized. I never intended to insult her, Carl, or the family – and I’ll just keep my thoughts on Carl’s looks to myself. Looks don’t really matter to Jazz anyway but then she’s in it for the emotion and the potential connection. Unlike me who’s more interested in someone fun, handsome, and reasonably intelligent. Yeah – probably superficial as hell to put good looking in my top three, but so what? Not like I’m out for a great love affair anyway.

                With things cleared up between me and Tavia dinner wasn’t all that uncomfortable an affair. We talked about needing to resolve things with Big Sean and made a plan to take care of that Thursday night. That’d put us in decent shape for taking care of hopefully both Stanton Marshal and Bonnie Lynn before the weekend was out. I spent the rest of the night working on the finishing touches for the security system and listening to the Pirates game. They were stomped by the freakin’ Nationals!


                June 10 (Thursday)

                Didn’t do much until this evening when we went to Sean’s pub. We needed to do a ceremony to help Sean move on now that Rosie’s out of the picture, but in order to do the ceremony we needed something personal of Sean’s. Luckily there were some artifacts in the pub that were supposed to be his. Alexia’s got pretty quick fingers so she was in charge of snagging something from the display while Nat and I distracted everyone by doing body shots. I know, sounds ridiculous but it worked – and I got some decent rum out of it, so yay? Jazz wasn’t impressed with out distraction and went upstairs as soon as she could. When we got up there she’d already talked to Sean and got him to agree it was time to go.

                Since he was Catholic in life we thought I might have a better chance of helping him move on. In a way I didn’t want to do it (not that I told anyone). Sean wasn’t exactly the type of victim I came back to help, but I figured that for a guy like Sean all you need is forgiveness and a willingness to accept your mistakes to move on. Between Jazz and I we nudged him onto the right path and he faded from sight in a way that was both incredibly peaceful but also energizing. I felt like we’d truly accomplished something.

                On the way home Jazz told me that Jean sent her some flowers today. I caught Alexia’s eye and she gave me a look that told me not to screw things up. No way was I gonna do something to make Jazz question the sincerity of Jean’s gift even if I thought it was lame that Alexia cheated and helped the dude. Jazz was happier than I think I’ve ever seen her, so I just embraced the whole thing and gave her a bit of encouragement. Of course then she had to start questioning the whole thing so I demanded that she just enjoy it. I think I said something like “Enjoy the Sweetness” – who says shit like that anyway? Me obviously, but geez.

                Back home we all hung out around the new bar Simon built and enjoyed a few drinks that Doc mixed up before heading for bed.


                June 11 (Friday)

                Tonight was a new moon so we took it easy during the day. I realized somewhere around one or two that I hadn’t actually asked Anders to be my date for the party. I gave him a call and asked – weird, I was a little nervous about the whole thing. But he accepted and was cool with the fact that the rest of his krewe weren’t invited. No way were we having Sophie in Nat’s vicinity, and Moire and I were gonna have us a conversation if she didn’t figure out that Anders is not hers in any capacity other than krewe bond. No need to risk having that conversation during the party we’ve been planning for a while. (ok so the others planned. I just put together the security system)

                When it was time, Alexia, Tavia, Simon, and I went into the room with the paintings and stood watch to make sure nothing came out of them. Jazz, Doc, and Nat were in the hall ready to lend assistance if needed but considering they were all susceptible to the vices the two paintings were attuned to – they stayed out of the way so they wouldn’t make it any easier for the Others to escape.

                Damn good thing too, a giant claw reached out of the Hanger in the Void painting but it couldn’t quite break through so we dodged a bullet for another month. Now, we sleep for tomorrow we party.

                End Session XXXIX

                First off, forgot to note before, between here and rpg.net we have exceeded 25,000 views. Thanks for that. Actually we are near 30,500 and are sure to pass that with this set of entries.

                It needs not be said of course that the players found the relationship conversations hilarious. And yes, Alexia intervened.

                Alexia talked to Mr. Mongler, who noted offhandedly they could speak to Cock Robin whenever, and that he was happy to come along. Apparently he dropped by to see Cock Robin and Cock Robin hid under the table though their entire conversation. Richard also bemoaned no one ever called with Dinosaur questions.

                The body shots stuff was also fun. It came up and apparently people ran with it.

                The Hanger in the Void almost got enough successes to break free, but not quite. It was only one success away, hence the issues.


                Not much else…passed a lot of time again, and tomorrow’s session we have party. Hope to see you when we discuss that bit of madness. Hope everyone enjoyed.
