                            Session XLII

                            Doc’s Log

                            Doc’s Log: 1415 Hours, Sunday, June 13, 2010

                            Well now this has been a fun morn...er afternoon. I got up about 1200 Hours and meditated. Took a little bit to settle but after last night I guess I shouldn’t be surprised. Rose came around looking for Snowflake just as I was finishing up. That was about 1330 Hours so I had a sneaking suspicious Snowflake was AWOL. Ronin confirmed he slipped out after most of us went to bed this morning. The only thing that made any sense was that he went to see his mystery woman. Now how in the hell can I be a good wingman if he doesn’t let me know what he’s planning? I mean I’m not thrilled with the idea of having to lie to Tavia but at least if I know what’s happening I can try to steer conversations away from touchy situations. Damn it...I’m having flash backs to when she first showed up. Why does it always come down to me having to ask my vampire friends to scramble my mortal friends’ brains?

                            Anyway, I called Snowflake to let him know his presence was missed. He really didn’t think through this at all. Now it’s not like I can throw stones, not being able to think on my feet for shit either, but he’s the sneaky occulty one. While I had him on the phone, I told Snowflake about going to Malard’s. He decided that it was a good idea to come home and go with me. I won’t lie and say that didn’t make me feel better. Then I got off the phone and went to look for cookies.

                            Cupcake was down there as always, but Nightshade caught me on her way out. She gave me a quick kiss and then went back upstairs. Can’t catch a break. There was a large box of Pocky on the table with a note that said To Doc in vaguely feminine handwriting. Tavia said she found it in the mailbox. Fabulous. Well I guess it’s better for Noriko to leave me candy than dead mice or worse. Tavia also informed me of the Pocky Tax in the House. I was truly not expecting that and had to laugh. I let her have some of the Pocky though. By then, Cupcake had a batch of chocolate chip cookies together in a container for me.

                            That was about the time I got the 911 text from Ronin. Now I won’t say that Ronin is the calmest person I know, but she is also far from gun shy. So when I got an emergency message from her, I bolted. When I got to her room, she pulled me in and closed the door. Apparently Tavia has been special today already. Ronin said she came around earlier asking to look at the security footage. Everything except for the were-porn was already erased so Tavia just kinda went blank and left. Ronin also told me that she caught Tavia at the computer after that trying to find things anyway. And Rose apparently thinks Tavia is looking to hunt down the women Snowflake danced with last night. Ronin let me know that she was encrypting the backups of the footage and taking them off site. Not a bad plan really. But I knew where this was going. The curse of being the adopted big brother to the Fishborns is that I’m the first line of contact for anyone that thinks little brother or little sister are going off the deep end. That this is usually a correct assumption is just decorations on the crazy cake. Thus I had to fill Ronin in on the “Snowflake Gone Wild” angle. It’s almost funny how all the social cluster fuck of my adopted siblings is suddenly my responsibility to sort out. Almost.

                            When Snowflake pulled in, I met him in the driveway with the cookies so we could just go straight over to Malard’s. Well that was the intention, but we started talking on the way. See Snowflake learned everything he knows about people from watching soap operas or something. Seriously. He admitted to me that he is WAY out of his depth with this whole situation. Apparently Snowflake did some soul searching at some point and decided that he really didn’t want to “be the man he was suppose to be.” That being a sociopathic cult leader with a mate so close in relation that they’d even look at him funny down in Alabama. I say that knowing damn well that some of the smartest people I went to training with came from ‘Bama. But still. Stereotypes work. Also I’m a terrible person. And easy to distract today I guess. I told him about Tavia’s latest actions and that other people are afraid she’s going to murder people. I told him I wasn’t afraid she’d do it, I knew she was going to do it just as soon as she figured out who she needed to kill. This is part of the reason why he’s not as into her apparently. We walked up and down the street several times talking about this before we finally turned and walked up Malard’s steps. And the requisite gunshot.

                            Malard was happy to see the cookies...and us. Angelique was there...though she was wearing a robe and a bra. She didn’t make any move to cover up so I didn’t let it be a big deal. It wasn’t bad to look at but it did make the visit a little awkward. It was also a little weird because I thought werewolves were forbidden to sleep together, and that’s kinda what it seemed like we interrupted. Not that I was going to call the old man on it. I thought I was going to piss the old man off when I explained what happened but then I realized he was convincing himself that it was completely normal for me to do something like that. He told me that sometimes you just have to slap a werewolf around to get them to keep in line. Angelique filled in to call his bluff as I couldn’t make any sound come out when my moth fell open. Apparently Erin is still very ...jealous? that she wasn’t able to fight against the Ivory Claws when they were in town. That I was involved in that just made it that much worse. Malard assured us it was more than forgiven. The talk of Erin only being able to last about eight seconds while sparring with Malard was taken to heart, though I doubt it was aimed at me. With all that taken care of, I mentioned after Erin had some time to calm down, I’d like to apologize to her directly. Then we headed back to the Bluffs.

                            We took the scenic route this time so we could talk more about Julian and how he plays into Snowflakes Grand Romance. When we finally got back, we split up and I ran interference. Tavia told me that someone had pilfered my Pocky. I gave her some more and tried to move the conversation toward how she was. That fell flat and then Chandra led her off to go upstairs. Chandra is not helping things on multiple levels. I do not want to have to kill two of my Krewe’s love interests.

                            After that I went to find Nightshade. I knew at the very least I could vent to her and get another perspective. She listened like always but she didn’t really have much insight to offer. Not surprising as I doubt many people would. Unfortunately she also didn’t have anything to say about last night. She always knows if I try to fish for things, so that wouldn’t have been good. I just let it go. Apparently I’m fine if I’m just trying to nail a werechick but having a relationship with a mostly human woman is nearly beyond me. Somewhere there is a cosmic entity laughing at me. Now I’m going to sign off and get ready for dinner.


                            Doc’s Log: 1945 Hours, Sunday, June 13, 2010

                            Spaghetti is always fun times at the table. Unfortunately, so many people were looking around the table with odd glances that it was not as fun as usual. Then Rose decided we didn’t have enough to deal with so she reminded us that we didn’t have every minute of the next week planned out yet. Now I’m just being grumpy, but she just doesn’t know or get or care about so many things that are happening … but that’s for the best. Anyway, we’re planning to jump right back into Shatina’s missing brother tomorrow and we now have an appointment with the Changeling Cock Robin for Tuesday evening. I can’t wait for that one. I was kinda zoning out after that. Too many things on my mind. Once we had a battle plans, I decided to hide in the Library for a while and see what I could learn about the political structures of our supernatural brethren. I am not planning to have another incident like last night.


                            Doc’s Log: 0350 Hours, Monday, June 14, 2010

                            I focused on the vampire institutions to begin with. Wow...that was an hour of very eye opening information. I’m SO glad we didn’t do anything to piss off the Sheriff now. After that I went up and helped Nightshade clean up around the DJ booth. As I was coming down, Rose grabbed me and let me know she was turning Bonnie Lyn’s wedding dress into a charm. I couldn’t help but tease her since no one else would be lining up to take a wedding dress, charm or not.

                            Like normal, I slipped out and headed back to the dance studio to work with Sebastian. I apparently haven’t made a notable enough effort on Nightshade, so I’m going to continue working on this whole culture thing. It’s also a great way for me to keep in shape and get more flexible. Thinking maybe if I alternate between dance practice and patrols, I’ll be doing pretty good.


                            Doc’s Log: 1020 Hours, Monday, June 14, 2010

                            Meditation went better than yesterday. I feel more at ease with the world and it only took an hour to center. That means I was able to have breakfast with the Krewe. Unfortunately that cosmic laughter was back again. I don’t know what was happening, but there was something weird going on between Chandra and Tavia. Chandra started making the grade school hand sign for having sex and I don’t think anyone knew where that came from. After that, I just left for work. Now I’m just working and drawing my weirdo dream image.


                            Doc’s Log: 1100 Hours, Monday, June 14, 2010

                            That laughter is getting louder.

                            Noriko came in after the last customer left. I tried to act like everything was okay...as I was hoping it was. She made it clear she was still not over Saturday night. After making some attempts at getting us to go out on patrol together, which I thought was a fair idea, she told me she loved and walked out. I was still blinking when she walked back in a few minutes later and apologized to me. I got her number so we could coordinate something for the patrols and she headed out again.

                            I called Nightshade immediately. I wanted her to know what was happening as it was making me feel very weird. And it’s always good just to talk to her. She told me to let the crush wear off before letting anything else happen. Then see how things go. I could tell that she still seemed to think I should just grab Noriko up and keep her as my stress relief. There’s alot of things wrong with that thought, but most of all it’s disappointing that she thinks it’s okay for me to do that.


                            Doc’s Log: 1335 Hours, Monday, June 14, 2010

                            Nightshade came over for lunch like normal. That was incredibly relaxing to have something that feels consistent right now. When she left, I went back to drawing. Though I did start working trying to draw Angelique. That will take more time. We’ll see if they start early with the ceremony today or not.


                            Doc’s Log: 1645 Hours, Monday, June 14, 2010

                            Yeap, I called that one. I’m waiting for the cab to pick me up so we can go track down someone that may not be in this plane of reality anymore. When I came in, they sent me up to get Nightshade. I walked to the door and heard she was talking to someone online. Since it was open I knocked on the door frame just to get her attention, but she called me into the room. She was doing the Skype thing with baby Nightshade. There was proper introductions and then I kinda got out of the way. Nightshade was out not long after that though. I can’t help with this ceremony so I’m writing this instead. We’ll see what Rose can get from the reading this time.


                            Jasmine’s Diary

                            Sunday, June 13, 2010, Evening

                            It has been a busy day, and this is the first chance I’ve had to write since I woke this morning. Though really, since I was keyed up after the party and didn’t get to sleep until close to 6:00 this morning, I actually slept until noonish. Most of the others got up about when I did, and we spent the day cleaning up. There was a lot of mess left in the aftermath of the party, not to mention all of the decorations to take down, so we had plenty to keep us busy.

                            Unfortunately, Natalie stayed locked in her room with Karen until almost dinner time. She did clean up her DJ booth later, but it would have been nice to have her helping us clean up the lion’s share of the mess elsewhere in the house. And it certainly would have perked up Doc’s spirits to have her around. He barely got to enjoy being with her during the party…

                            To make matters worse, Simon was out on some errand and didn’t return until mid-afternoon. Tavia seemed unhappy about that, which made me worry a bit.

                            In any event, the cleaning went well and the house is back to normal.

                            Before we started cleaning, though, I drew Alexia aside and reminded her that we needed to talk to Chandra. A good night’s sleep had restored her bright and cheerful disposition, but as soon as I mentioned the issue with her girlfriend, she became glum again. But we had to deal with it, and so we took Chandra into the parlour to have a chat.

                            So, the situation is rather more tenuous than I thought. Chandra has stopped feeding on Trinh because Alexia asked, but she badly wants to turn Trinh into a Penanggalan. Needless to say, I was appalled! We have no right to inflict our own supernatural curses on others, especially not turning someone into a creature like a vampire! Trinh deserves to be able to live out her mortal life happily, not have it stolen from her! And I will not allow her to be snatched from the karmic cycle and forced into an existence of stagnant immortality like that!

                            Chandra was insulted at the way I felt. She thinks her life is better as a Penanggalan, and perhaps she’s right, in her way. But she’s still been denied the fulfillment of her dharma and I don’t believe there’s any justification for dragging Trinh into the dangerous supernatural world in which we live. She’s already experienced enough of that with her grandmother’s ghost.

                            Chandra agreed to avoid Trinh and not to turn her into a Penanggalan, but she made it very clear that she only did so because Alexia wanted it. So after Chandra left the room, I told Alexia that she was accountable for Chandra’s actions. I insisted that she become more responsible in that regard, as she has a history of caving in to Chandra’s every whim.

                            If Chandra continues to harm Trinh or changes her into a Penanggalan, things will not go well. In any event, I fear my friendship with Chandra has become broken in a way that I may not be able to repair.

                            Gwen happened upon Alexia and I as we were finishing our conversation, so I explained the situation to her. Gwen’s reaction was quite bombastic. She agreed with me completely, and told Alexia that she would kill Chandra if she turned Trinh. That sounds a bit harsh, but it may come to that, and if it does, I will need to steel myself for doing what must be done. It seems it was the day for that, given what I later learned of Tavia.

                            Over dinner, we made plans for the days to come. It’s a huge relief to have resolved everything surrounding the party and laying Bonnie-Lynn to rest. But we still have a lot to do.

                            After dinner, I went upstairs to pack up Bonnie-Lynn’s things and clean out her room, and to prepare her corpse and coffin for burial. We’re planning to bury her in the graveyard behind the house tomorrow. Tavia was also Bonnie’s friend, so she went with me to help. It didn’t take long. I’ve kept Bonnie’s diary, which I plan to read later tonight, and also her wedding dress, which I’ll turn into a Tear-Stained charm by scribing some passages from her diary into the lining.

                            While we were cleaning out the room, I took the opportunity to ask Tavia what was bothering her, as it was clear she was upset about Simon. She became quite distant when I asked, and after a moment’s hesitation, told me she would rather not discuss it. It didn’t press her.

                            I asked Doc about it later, though, and he explained that Tavia was jealous of the other women Simon had danced with and that she feared Simon was involved with one of them. It became fairly clear to me that Tavia would not hesitate to murder these girls, especially if Simon was involved with one of them. In fact, I gather she spent part of the day trying to find out exactly who they were so that she could do just that!

                            I love Tavia. Really, I do. But from day one she has proven herself to be dangerous. Possibly too dangerous to be left wandering around. Certainly most the time she’s simply adorable, if trying at times. But if her unhealthy obsession is as psychotic as it seems, then I cannot allow her to simply do as she wishes. If she is actually capable of murdering these girls (and I believe she is), she might need to be put away in a mental institution that can help her. My fear is that locked away in such a place, she might be given free reign to paint more of her vice-summoning paintings, inviting more outsiders into the world.

                            I don’t know what to do, and I fear what might need to be done. But as with Chandra, I’ve steeled myself to do what must be done if it comes to it. That said, Gwen is much less patient with these things than I am, and might take things in her own hands… I wish I knew how to help Tavia get over her obsession with Simon, but I’m not good with normal people, and I have no idea how to fix a damaged mind like hers.


                            Monday, June 14, 2010, Afternoon

                            I went to train Jean this morning. He was glad to see me back and well again, and I thanked him for the flowers. It was such a sweet gesture! I felt a bit lousy, though, thanking him for the flowers when my thoughts have really been on Julian since Saturday.

                            I met with Mongler after training, and he’s agreed to take us to see Cock Robin tomorrow evening, after he leaves work.

                            Shortly after I got home, Simon asked to talk to me. He hasn’t really opened up to me much since we’ve know each other, so I was quite pleased when he decided to ask my advice on his troubles with Tavia. He admitted that he was seeing another girl, and he’d been with her yesterday morning. Of course they were sleeping together. Why wouldn’t they? It certainly seems to be the order of the day in this household full of loose people.

                            But…I took pains not to express my displeasure over that to Simon, as I wanted to help him, and I wanted him to feel comfortable talking to me about things like this. He explained that he was seeing another girl and that he really doesn’t feel comfortable with Tavia. Her obsession with him is stifling, and he wants the freedom to choose who he is with instead of being forced into an arranged marriage with a psychotically obsessive girlfriend.

                            I encouraged him. I want him to break away from Tavia. I’ve never approved of their relationship for many reasons, and psychotic obsession is certainly not a good foundation for love. And I think Simon should find romance on his own terms, which seems to be what he’s trying to do. So for what it’s worth, I told him I approved of finding someone on his own, though perhaps not of the sly way he was hiding it from Tavia instead of breaking things off with her in advance.

                            That said, dealing with Tavia is not easy. She is psychotic, after all. I asked Simon if he wanted me to talk to her, and was quite relieved when he said he’d take care of it himself.

                            Well, I think Doc’s just arrived home, so I need to go down and prepare the Finding ceremony to search for Shatina’s brother.


                            Footnotes

                            Of One Mind

                            The Drowned Bride and I have somehow become closer. I didn’t really realize it until my meditations this morning, when I reached out with my psyche to find her and discovered that she was already there. It was almost like suddenly becoming self-aware. I could still recognize her thoughts and feelings as hers, not mine…but there wasn’t really much difference between them.

                            I’ve always known this would happen whenever I find love again. I haven’t exactly gotten there yet, though. But I think that participating in Bonnie and Stanton’s blissfully romantic passing and my own excitement over having met Julian have pleased the Bride. And I think the longing I felt as I drifted to sleep last night only endeared us to one another more, for she has dwelt in constant longing for well over a century, and understands it far better than I do

                            It scares me a bit, though. The Bride represents deathly resolve and the desire for love. She is romance and longing, but what will happen if that longing is ever fulfilled? What will we be then? What if the longing can never be fulfilled?


                            Names

                            When I was discussing the Chandra situation with Gwen, I mentioned that Chandra had called me Xiao Ming (my Chinese name) rather than Jasmine (my English name) to underscore how serious she was, or maybe to create a sense of distance between us…

                            So Gwen started to wonder if I wanted to be called by my “real” name, and asked if she should call me Xiao Ming instead of Jasmine. I’d never really thought about it before. In America and New Zealand, I’ve always been Jasmine. It’s what Richard called me, except when he wanted convey a sense of intimacy. These days, only my family in Taiwan calls me Xiao Ming anymore, so I told Gwen that Jasmine was fine.


                            Julian

                            I asked Simon a bit about Julian this afternoon. They’ve been friends for a long time, apparently, and Simon speaks highly of him. I wondered why he’d never had Julian over to the house before, and suggested that he invite him for dinner some time.

                            The Bride is almost giddy over my ulterior motive for the suggestion.


                            Plans
                            (Monday) 1. Arrange meeting with Cock Robin for Tuesday evening.

                            (Monday) 2. Perform Finding ceremony to track down Shatina’s brother.

                            (Tuesday) 3. Meet with Cock Robin about Shatina and fetches.

                            (?) 4. Deal with Shatina and her brother based on what we learn from the Finding ceremony and Cock Robin.

                            (?) 5. Visit the Athenaeum in the Underworld to find information on the Strix

                            (?) 6. Present information on the Strix to the vampires in order to gain their assistance in dealing with other problems.


                            A Ring?

                            I’m not sure, but I think I caught Tavia and Chandra gesturing about a ring discreetly at breakfast. Could Chandra be planning to propose to Alexia?

                            Normally I’d be thrilled about it, and to some degree I can’t help but be excited since the two of them seem to love each other quite a lot. But they’ve only known each other less than two months, and I can’t help but fear that they’re still in the infatuation stage of their relationship and that it’s too early for them to even consider marriage.

                            And right now…I’m worried about Chandra and Tavia. And I’m worried about their ability to switch from being charmingly flighty to deadly serious in an instant. In Chandra’s case in particular, it’s come to take on a darker cast, like her bubbly persona is a manipulative deception. I know that’s not true, or at least I hope not, but I can’t shake the impression.

                            Gwen’s Journal

                            June 13 (Sunday)

                            No time to record things yesterday, but now that I’ve had a few hours of sleep and some coffee I’m pretty good to go. Just finished going over the security tapes from the party. I gotta admit, it was a pretty big success, one that I think made a pretty major impression on some folks.

                            I guess I’ll just record everything here now. Yesterday we all got up a bit late – not a big surprise considering we had the demon paintings to guard Friday night but still. I spent most of the day testing the security system but when it was done I helped Jazz and Alexia with some of the decorating. The party started at 9 so the bouncers and bartenders were there around 8:30. I did my best to make sure they knew what was and wasn’t allowed – last thing we needed was a raid with a bunch of underage goths high or drunk on the property.

                            Nat was DJing and she had a trio of goth girls hanging out in her booth. They were somehow part of her show I guess. I mean didn’t really get it. Considering her booth was above the dancefloor and off to one side of Simon’s throne why have three chicks rubbing up on her while she spins tunes? Where’s the entertainment factor? For anyone other than her that is. None of them were involved with her, though I got the impression all of ‘em would’ve been happy to be. But if she was wanting some affection or adoration for her own personal pleasure I’m pretty sure Karen and Doc would’ve been happy to provide. I kind of see it that way too. Karen provides the adoration and Doc the affection and stability. Anyway – I’m going off on a tangent that’s none of my freakin’ business.

                            Anders showed up before the doors officially opened, which didn’t bother me in the least. He looked great – not quite gothed out but still appropriately attired and hot. =) He didn’t seem to mind the fact that he’d wind up spending more than half the night in the security room. Would’ve been more awesome if Chandra hadn’t been in there too.

                            I gotta admit, I didn’t get why Chandra was there in any capacity. I know she’s dating Alexia – got our Sunshine wrapped around her finger even, but she’s a vampire. One that hasn’t been introduced to the head honchos in the city and if she runs into one of them there could be problems. So why the hell was she sticking around the Bluffs when we knew good and well vamps would probably show up? The claim was she’d be there to help if something went pear shaped, but you know what? Her being there was a danger. An added threat that wasn’t necessary and if she cared about Alexia at all I think she would’ve accepted that and gone back to her store for the night. Guess that’s a little too logical for the crazy vampire but whatever.

                            We expected one or two people to show up just because they wanted to see the inside of the Bluffs, but I did not like it when I caught this chick walking the lay lines in the house. Jazz actually talked to her since she recognized her as a AMC student. Her name was Maria and her aura was pretty wild. She sparkled for godssake! Not like that Twilight pansy-ass Stalker Vampire – but it was like someone glitterbombed her aura. I know that just indicates she uses magic, but seriously? What’s even weirder is the fact that her body’s never died, but her soul? 20 or 30 deaths easy. How does that happen? And what the hell is she???

                            I left the party to the others for the most part but I had to make sure I was conspicuously there at times. While I wasn’t all Draven’ed out like Doc (who looked bad ass lemme tell ya) or as ghostlike as Jazz (she was flowing from one shadow to the next – was pretty awesome looking actually) I was the one in charge of the security – I needed to make sure people recognized that someone was watching them. Of course Simon was watching everyone through his boneyard while he lorded over everyone from his throne, but they didn’t know it. With me watching from the landing and the cameras, those that cared knew they wouldn’t be able to get away with anything.

                            I’m bouncing all over the place – you’d think I’d be able to keep some sort of chronological order given I can still refer back to the tapes if I needed to – but screw it. I prefer relying on my brain even if it is a little scrambled. A vamp I didn’t know showed up not long after Truman, Rebecca, and Neon. They call themselves Anarchs but they seem pretty cool to me. Truman said the new vamp’s name was Lacey Hart and she was friends with Maxwell, the leader of the city. I decided I’d be the one to make contact this time and it was weird. She acted like she knew me, but no surprise, I had no recollection of her. And I think I would’ve remembered someone like her. She was lovely and just had a way about her. I’m not saying I was attracted to her but for the first time that I’m aware of I can honestly say I looked twice at another woman. She was just neat to be around you know? She was charming. Not like Anders but still. I went back to my guy pretty quickly. Not because I was running from Lacey, I just wanted to be with my date. Unfortunately that’s when we discovered that Chandra’d left the security room. With at least four, maybe more vampires in the house.

                            Alexia found her and based on what I could hear through the curl Chandra convinced Alexia that she was safe to be off and floating along around the party guests. The invisibility thing she can do I guess. But that didn’t mean one of the vamps wouldn’t see her. We don’t know what all they can do after all. I let Alexia know how bad an idea this was, but considering she was already agitated by whatever Chandra had said it didn’t really make much of a difference. Alexia was in a bad mood for the rest of the night and that’s just not natural.

                            The party was well underway when a couple of Doc’s werewolf friends showed up, none of us have a problem with them so that was no big deal. Except not long after that Zed and two others from Mallard’s pack came in too. Now the Bluffs’ technically in Mallard’s pack’s territory but it’s Simon’s damn house. Doc got to them first and told them they were welcome but so were his friends from the other pack. One of the were’s got pissed at him for it and took it as an insult or something. Pretty sure Simon smoothed things over later but what the hell man?

                            I get you don’t go into someone else’s territory, but the Bluffs is OUR territory and we damn well say who is and isn’t welcome. Simon’s word is law here – literally. So that werechick needed a serious attitude adjustment but apparently we weren’t giving those out last night. No, instead we played nice and Doc left them alone. Jazz warned Deidre and Tanya that they were there but they were cool, they weren’t interested in causing problems. Neither was Noriko when she showed up…well, not the gonna get violent kind of problems anyway.

                            At one point I caught her stalking something or someone in the lounge and Doc went to check it out. Guess he thought he was being cute baiting the werecat with a curtain tie-back but trust me, he did not get the reaction he was looking for! Noriko pounced him and I’m not sure she wouldn’t have succeeded in having her way with him if we weren’t connected through the ear curls. I started up a stream of “You’re not seriously…dude, my eyes…Nat’s gonna kill you” sort of crap and he jumped back. Let Noriko down gently from what I could hear but I was also in the process of picking on him as Nat asked for the video footage for later funtimes.

                            That chick ain’t right sometimes I swear. Still, hey, if it helped her see Doc was the one for her then by all means. She could have the footage. I think me agreeing to that embarrassed Doc and disgusted Jazz. But what the hell right? I don’t know maybe it was tasteless to agree to it, but I kind of want Nat to get jealous. Maybe if that happens she’ll give Doc the attention he deserves. He’s a damn good guy even if he does piss me off on occasion (seriously, who doesn’t?). And good guys are in short supply in the world. I was OK with this whole triangle deal she, Karen, and Doc set up to begin with but it seems to me like Doc gets the short end of the stick most of the time so I’m getting less cool with it. Anytime Nat starts falling apart she goes to Karen. If Karen gets jealous then she gets like hours or maybe days of Nat’s focus. What does Doc get? To bust his ass so he can impress his girl with a romantic dance at her goth ball and she just sort of “Meh’s” it away and returns to her booth. I know. I know. Judge not…but damnit, I am SO judging!

                            And yeah, I jumped ahead in the night but dude? My damn journal. I can bounce around if I want to. See, it was getting late, like around two or so I think? And Doc asked for the dance he’d requested. Nat seemed kind of reluctant, but put on one of her compositions (again as he requested) and came down to the dancefloor. A circle was formed, kind of like when BonnieLynn & Marshal danced (I’ll get to that in a minute) and Simon made the spotlights appear – considering the Sheriff (sort of an enforcer for the vamp big wigs) of the city just showed up that might not’ve been the best idea – but what do I know? Anyway it was a gorgeous dance. You could tell Doc really worked hard because three weeks ago? He was tripping over his own feet and stepping on toes. He’s been practicing. Jazz and I were both feeling the love considering how much effort he put into that but once it was done Nat just went back to work. Not even a kiss.

                            Y’know, considering how many of the weres are lusting after Doc maybe he should take ‘em up on it. I’m not sure Nat’s worth it in the romance department. Maybe she’s too enamored with women to truly give this thing with Doc a go? I’m pretty sure she’d say she’s giving it her best shot and Doc would tell me to butt out if I ever said anything (which I won’t…for now) but anyway, I’m beginning to think they might not be the best match.

                            Speaking of matches I totally made one last night! Jazz was kind of sad (all those goths, and the sad one is the Olympic athlete – so weird) and she was talking with Alexia about how she felt so alone and a bit ignored. OK – first – she was a freakin’ shadow most of the night and second – the woman is not approachable! She’s a sweetheart and she cares deeply but she looks so remote. I can tell you men are intimidated by that sort of look, so she kind of got herself into that position all on her own. Still, given I overheard all this through the ear curls I asked Anders if he would dance with her. He said he would but thought she’d be uncomfortable dancing with my date. (side note: I kind of thought he was gonna say boyfriend or something but he stopped at the last minute – probably reading too much into things but is he looking to make this thing more…exclusive?) Anyway I sort of cursed and had to agree with him so I headed downstairs pulled Simon’s earcurl out (made him jump which was kind of funny) and asked him if his friend (the guy, not the mysterious chick) was single. He seemed kind of confused at first but said he was. When I explained I wanted him to get the guy to dance with Jazz he readily agreed.

                            I stuck around so I could see if things worked out. Honestly I was only wanting her to get a dance, nothing major, but about the time Simon talked to his friend that Sheriff guy came in and Jazz went to the shadows to track him. I couldn’t get a read off him, but he gave me a look…yeah, that look like I give people when I’m reading them. Not sure what he got off it, but he didn’t make any threatening moves and our vampire friends took off so they didn’t come into contact with him so nothing to worry about.

                            That being the case I badgered Jazz until she reappeared and just as she made the snarky comment about no one lining up to see her Julian approached. SCORE! The look on her face…I think I might blow up the video still from that moment. Shock doesn’t begin to describe it, HA! Then she went off and danced with him…and danced with him again…and then let him get her a drink and they talked for the rest of the night. I so rock in finding her a dude that seems to get her! *fistpump*

                            Oh yeah – I almost forgot – Bonnie Lynn and Marshal had a beautiful moment. Doc took care of Marshal and Jazz played ladies maid to Bonnie. They escorted them to the stairs, they met on the landing and Marshal took Bonnie Lynn’s hand and led her to the floor. Simon took care of the effects, Nat had the perfect music for them and everyone there could tell it was a truly special moment for them. Good for Marshal, he stuck around, he stayed to guard the woman he loved, he deserved the happy ending.

                            When Jazz married the two of them at dawn they faded away, moving on to share their afterlife and for that moment I think we all felt pretty good. Jazz was actually happy, I mean the smile reached her eyes and there was no sadness in her eyes. I think I was happier about that than anything.


                            June 14 Monday – Midnight

                            My god we have got drama in the Bluffs!

                            My hand was cramping and my coffee cup was empty after I finished writing about the party so I went downstairs to get a refill and give the writing a break. I came into the kitchen to hear Alexia proclaiming that she was responsible! (yes, you could hear the exclamation point) Jazz had her “this is serious and I’m displeased” look on her face. Damnit all to hell we just had her happy less than eight hours ago! I had to know what put that look on her face so I asked what was going on. Alexia didn’t want to talk about it so I refilled my coffee and Jazz went back upstairs with me to fill me in.

                            It seems that Chandra’s disappearing act at the party happened because she’s been feeding off Trinh, Jazz’s student they helped out while I was road tripping. Alexia asked Chandra to stop this a while back but our resident blood sucker is a bit obsessed with her, so she apparently hung around her until she left the Bluffs Saturday night. That obsession? It’s caused by Chandra’s compulsion to make Trinh a Penanggalan like her. Do I really need to say how much of a bad idea this is? If Chandra does anything to that girl I will kill her. It’s that simple. I get she’s a different kind of vampire so the whole stake through the heart thing might not work for her (does it work for the more common vamps? I’m going to need to investigate) but one way or another she’s toast if she does it. Apparently, according to Jazz the only reason Chandra isn’t indulging her need to procreate is Alexia. Since Alexia asked her not to she won’t do it, but Jazz says the implication is clear. The minute Alexia gives the OK, Trinh is Chandra’s very own bloodshake. And the sad thing is? Alexia will give the OK. She’s a marshmellow when it comes to Chandra. She can’t say no to her for long, she enjoys making her happy and normally I’d think that’s pretty cool – but lets not forget Chandra’s a damn vampire! She acts like a brainless idiot but she’s no dummy. We have no idea how long she’s been on this earth and she knows exactly how to play Alexia.

                            And you know something? Even ignoring the fact that I’m one step away from going Buffy on that chick there’s all those other vampires out there. I’m pretty sure they’d notice if a newbie vampire showed up. And I can’t see that going well.

                            It’s not a great situation but Jazz and I decided we needed to talk to Nat, Simon, and Doc about the situation. They need to know about it so they can keep an eye on Alexia and make sure she doesn’t pull an “Oh, you’re so sweet, of course you can drain that kid dry!”

                            We tabled everything since Nat was holed up with Karen (I’ve been on my soapbox on that topic, not going there again…today) and neither of us had seen Doc and Simon had left early that morning. I didn’t want to be cooped up in my room all day so I went downstairs with Jazz to help with the cleanup efforts. No way were we getting a cleaning crew in this house so it was up to us. I was picking up trash in the ballroom when Tavia and Chandra came through. I did good not threatening Chandra. But then Tavia asked to see the security tapes. Well hell, I’d already wiped them so I told her so. She nodded and continued on as if nothing was said but Chandra stopped and waited until she was out of the room before telling me that Tavia never needs to see the footage as she’s certain the girls Simon danced with at the party were in danger.

                            OK, now let’s put this into perspective. The bloodsucking vampire is worried about the girls Simon danced with and Tavia the Unstable is trying to be casual about getting a better look at those girls. Uh, hell no. C’mon now, I’m not an idiot, I made backups to the footage just in case. But my file system is not exactly logical to anyone but me so I didn’t figure she’d find them if she decided to go looking. Just in case though I went up to the security room and sure enough she was there and staring at the computer.

                            I don’t know if she even knows how to use a computer but I was not about to let Tavia go messing with the state of the art system I put together. I explained once more that the footage was gone but I don’t think she believed me. Her eyes went kind of flat and emotionless. When she left I shut the door texted Doc a 911 and waited for him to get there. Well I told him about Tavia and he said Simon hooked up with someone else but he didn’t know how serious the hookup was. He also told me that Simon and Tavia were together – it isn’t just in Tavia’s head like we kind of thought it was. I mean I knew he was playing the part of lord of the manor with her as his lady, but I didn’t realize it was more than just placating her. He’s involved with her and apparently someone else as well – isn’t that just perfect? Since Doc’s taken on this whole big brother role for both Simon and Tavia I suggested he talk to Tavia and try to get her to get off the murderous rampage. We’ll see if it works. When he left you better believe I encrypted and transferred the backups onto a flash drive and pocketed it. It’s going to the office – or maybe a safe deposit box tomorrow. The security computer is now password protected to the point that I’m pretty sure I’m the only one that will be getting into that system any time soon.

                            Simon got back a bit before dinner and Doc drafted him and some of Alexia’s cookies into going over to Mallard’s so he could apologize for the insult to Erin. Total crap as far as I’m concerned but hey – I’m not the “face” or the one that has to play nice in this Krewe. I’m here to protect the rest of them and make sure the ghosts are able to move on to their just rewards, end of story. Over dinner Doc told us that Mallard accepted the apology and Doc seemed to think he’d be talking to Erin about the situation as well. I hope it’s to give her a smackdown.

                            Simon left again after dinner. He said he had to get some things from the store and Alexia gave him a list of things as well. Nat went to clean up the dj area and Doc headed to the library to research something. Jazz and Tavia decided to clean up Bonnie Lynn’s room so I took the opportunity to take off and go have a beer and watch the game with some folks that would be blessedly drama free. Cops. But damnit the Tigers beat the Pirates by one freakin’ run. Time for bed, least in my dreams the Pirates are already in the world series.


                            Monday, June 14 – Lunch

                            Uneventful morning at the office but the backups are tucked away and safe. The highlight was email flirting with Anders, he can be quite talkative when he feels like it. We’re giving the Finding Ritual this afternoon. Hopefully will get a bead on Shatina’s brother. If not, at least we’re going to meet Cock Robin tonight, he should be able to give us a better idea about what to do with these fetches. And now…lunch.

                            End Session XLII

                            Dramadramadramadramadrama

                            First note, Jasmine was more on the money then Doc was about Chandra and Tavia's hand signals. This would explain Doc's players reaction though.

                            I'd note that Old Man Malard's name is less...ducky...then Gwen's spelling. I'll also note the meeting with Cock Robin was slated for Tuesday, not Monday...that will be important later.

                            So people seem to be having it out for people's SOs. That's fascinating. Certainly creating tension within the Krewe. I asked Doc's player how it felt to be the sanest member of the Krewe. He said Doc was cool with it but he was terrified....he along with Natalie being the PTSD sufferers.

                            Chandra's discussion with Alexia and Jasmine was pretty extensive, moreso then Jasmine recorded in terms of their issues especially with pregnant women (and Chandra's attempts to avoid pregnant women) and some pretty serious love declarations (Chandra said she would rather have been stillborn then never meet Alexia, and that meant becoming a vampire).

                            Natalie actually spent more of the first day after asleep then anything else. Chemicals did a number on her body the day before, Psyche resistance or no.

                                ournals time! As per, most arrived last minute. No Gwen journal this time, her player has threatened to…we may get a combo journal or she may send it individually…if she does I will add it here. Anyway, we ready?

                                Session XLIII

                                Doc’s Log

                                Doc’s Log: 0745 Hours, Wednesday, June 16, 2010

                                Well this has been exciting and informative. In much the same way as learning about new artillery while being in the firing line is exciting and informative. I think the Owlspider left bigger holes though. I’m getting ahead of myself...and I might also still be a little drunk. We’ll see.

                                Rose got the Finding ceremony to work this time and did so very well. She had a strong sense of what direction Jamie was in so we loaded up with Ronin and Snowflake driving before heading out. Unsurprisingly, I noticed we were heading into the shitty part of town. We never get to deal with bizarre scary things in the ritzy part of town. Anyway, we pulled up to an area surrounded by and old rusty fence. The fence wasn’t locked so Cupcake opened the gate and we headed in.

                                I’m no urban planner but when we immediately started seeing bookshelves overgrown with thorny vines, I was pretty sure we skipped Kansas altogether. None of the books looked weathered or water marked, Cupcake even pulled one off the shelf. Nothing happened so we asked our compass where North was. Rose said we had pulls from two directions but that the gate we came in was gone. Now don’t ask why we all had to turn around to verify she wasn’t making it up, but all we could see was down the same path we were standing on. No sign of our cars or anything about the normal world. Poda Kansas. That was about the time Ronin started bugging out. Rose said she thought we were in the Hedge, the magical otherworld of the Changelings. Ronin never really pays attention to the Faerie stuff, so when Rose said Hedge in her accent (we were rattled, accents happen) Ronin heard “Hidge” and had no idea what that referred to. After a few minutes of almost comical repetition, I finally stepped and translated hoping we could focus on getting out. So while we’re all looking around for the Yellow Brick Road, Cupcake tries to use her phone. That’s when she hears someone already on the other end asking where we are. He told her something about not worrying because he’d find us. If he said anything about her little dog too, I missed that part. Likely because Cupcake lost her shit about that time. It was more than a little disconcerting to be in a place where none of the normal rules apply. I mean the Dominions have the Old Laws and the Underworld has the Rivers, so there’s also some control. I thought maybe Snowflake could just zone out and look around to find another way out, so I said as much. His trance went weird though as he didn’t just go into a trance but went catatonic and started drooling. I snapped him out of it eventually and made sure he was still as together as when we started. Snowflake said that when he looked around it just went on forever. Then he said he wanted to try something else. This time I stayed by him to monitor...not sure what I thought I could do other than slap him to get him to come to, but still. Snowflake was able to look around a little more and found these human-sized, spider-mouthed owls in robes tending to the books on the shelves. In a different section was a blood-covered horse, just walking around. So many cowboy jokes now but it was terrifying at the time. Then he found the truly horrible creatures we were trying to mentally prepare for; whispering wolves with children’s faces, all looking for something…likely us.

                                Now we had the lay of the land in as much as we were willing to let Snowflake sacrifice what’s left of his mind to find out. We had to figure out what to do with this recon intel. The Dog let me know that there was no Twilight here. Apparently it’s an all in one kinda deal. The others seemed to come to that same conclusion. That’s about the time Rose unlocked her Primeval Caul and went all Cat Girl. There was a slight glow coming from Ronin’s eyes as she started looking around. I’m pretty sure she supertuned her senses because she started hearing where things were before anyone else. She found one of the upright walking owls, with spider mandibles and eight eyes so I decided to try and talk to it. It looked at us but didn’t seem to care. I tried to speak with it in English but that didn’t get very far. Then I unlocked the Primeval Marionette. I didn’t think I could control it, but it helps when talking to animals. And it worked this time too. It gave what it even said were bad directions for a way to get out. Not the first left, but one of the others. Polite conversation kept it talking a little. Apparently dreams help them make these books. I saw it spin one before shelving it, while it ate another before we started talking. It said that books were filled with knowledge. I looked at a few but they were written in a language and scripting style I’d never seen before. Probably some crazy dream language only Changelings can read.

                                I thanked the Spiderowl and moved ahead based on his directions. We skipped the first left altogether, but noticed a big freakish tree on the right with purple fruit the size of my head. I don’t know why we even thought about it, but we determined as a group that it would not be a good idea to eat any. Maybe it’s because we didn’t have any food or had no idea how long we’d be struck there. You know, little things. Then we came to section of shelves with books scattered all over the ground. For a second, I had a mental image of the Howling Dead charging through the silent halls of the Underworld library. I didn’t really have time to say anything because the ground started rumbling and this car-sized spider with two eyes and an owl’s beak crawled up in front of us. Five more the size of Great Dane’s scuttled out as well. It was weird because they looked like they were trying to be as quiet as something that makes the ground rumble could be. Made me think there was something more bad ass nearby they didn’t want to deal with.

                                I’m not sure if it was because I had the Primeval Marionette unlocked or just stupid luck (ha, luck witch has bad luck) but the big Owlspider decided to attack me first. I didn’t have any Shroud up yet, so it would have torn out my liver if I hadn’t Bulwarked the hit. Still hurt like taking shrapnel though. I immediately put up my Primeval Shroud at that point. Which was good considering the damn thing bit into me again right after that. That didn’t hurt nearly as bad. More like a knife wound. But it did piss me off, so I grew my own pointy claws from my Shroud, slashed the hell out of it, and snarled like a real beast. Then I channeled Plasm into my body to make myself stronger and tougher. I could feel my muscles ripple and swell just before it hit me again, but this time it was really minor. What I didn’t think about at the time was that I was tapping myself out too fast. Ronin finished it off with her Stillness Rage. I mean I guess that’s what she did. She didn’t make any sound and suddenly pieces of the thing sliced off like daggers were going crazy on it. Between us, we put it down but that still left the “baby” ones.

                                I had been pretty busy with the big one, and really didn’t think about what it could do to me. I was just keeping it from hurting the others until we could take it down. The others were dealing with the smaller beasts and honestly having a much better time of it. I mentioned that I was going through Plasm too fast before, but I didn’t really realize until I turned to fight the rest of this nest. I got a glancing shot off one just before it took a chunk out of my arm. But instead of white smoke, all I had was real blood running out. Rose had been handling things as one was down and several others were looking bad. One was kind of swimming in the dirt very slowly and Snowflake was causally walking away so I turned and ripped into it. By the time, I looked back they had finished with the rest too. At that point, Plasm and adrenaline weren’t keeping me up anymore...and neither did my legs. I was a pile of steaming white smoke and blood. I tore up my shirt to bind my wounds in hopes that I could stop the bleeding, then noticed that Cupcake and Ronin needed some healin’ done too. I didn’t have the Plasm to even heal me, but I could make us all stop bleeding so that was the first order. Well after getting me on my feet again anyway. I was the worst of us, though Ronin wasn’t far behind me and Cupcake was just caught off guard but it was still not a pretty sight. We knew we couldn’t just sit around and wait since the wolves were out there so Ronin and Rose helped me move til Ronin heard something again.

                                Rose went to scout it out since it was off to the left of the path. She came back a little bit later and said that we’d want to go that way, but we really shouldn’t. No one really felt like arguing the point so we kept moving. This time I was able to walk on my own at least so we organized with Rose and Cupcake up front, Nightshade and Snowflake in the middle, and Rose and me in the back. This seemed like the best formation right now given our list of injuries. The next left came up, but Rose found a lake with a sign that said “Beware of “ and had a huge jagged bite taken out at the bottom. Clearly not the way, so we kept moving. I was feeling better as we went, but I knew I’d be wet paper if we got into any real fight. The next left we found Rose didn’t find anything but more books so we headed that way. Moving a little ways in, we started hearing books slamming together. When we looked behind, books were flying off the ground into a humanoid shape. It seems silly now, but we tried to talk to this one too. It growled and started pulling more books off the vine-wrapped shelves and getting bigger. That was NOT what we needed. But things got even more special because Ronin heard something else coming from the way we had been and still should have been going. Rose immediately went to check it out.

                                We were slowly backing up and trying to figure out what the hell we could do to a huge pile of books when Ronin got a weird look on her face and turned back in the direction Rose ran off. She said something about Rose found Mongler. The next thing I know, there’s a Tyrannosaurus Rex with beefy human arms, wielding this massive great axe made from what looked like dinosaur teeth running straight for us with Rose racing to keep up. Rose was waving us off so we didn’t attack and it ran right past us. This thing was like twelve foot tall and roaring. I didn’t really want to deal with that ever so I was glad to know it was on our side. When I turned back, it was the Battle of the Colossi. The Book Beast had grown from all the extra books and DinoMan was just huge to start with. We were beaten, bruised, and bloody but there was no way we were going to just run like Mongler was yelling for us to do. I still had my claws, Ronin had her Rage, and Nightshade smiled and held out a lighter to Cupcake. Rose was up in the fight already. We knew what to do.

                                I’m not proud of destroying knowledge and I’d never sanction a book burning, but in this one case those books held knowledge that could kill. We walked away from the smoldering pile with Mongler, or Grin or whatever the hell his nickname is. But the weird started creeping back in again. Mongler said it was lucky we went missing on a day we were suppose to meet with him. I was confused. Rose never gets her schedules mixed up and she specifically said that we were meeting Mongler tomorrow. That’s when we learned that we’d lost a day and it was now Tuesday night. Eventually we came to a spot that he knew something about and opened a door into his office. It was about midnight or so on Tuesday according to the clock in his office. Though Mongler looked like Mongler again and that big ass axe looked like a normal walking stick. Ronin could still see something as she briefly started tracing out where the axe was. Guess she could still see through the Illusion or something. That’s good to know.

                                We realized that the cars were still in the shit hole where we left them. Ronin, Nightshade, Snowflake, Rose, and Cupcake’s phones start receiving notifications for everyone that was freaking out looking for them. My phone was silent. I knew that the one that should be worried about me was right there with me the whole time and I had protected her as best I could. Still even Mongler had a call from Gretchen waiting and he hadn’t been gone for almost two days. I’m not entirely sure why it weighed on me so much, but I suddenly knew what it meant to have a heavy heart. Thankfully I was pulled from my emoboy thoughts by the realization that we couldn’t go home yet until we had the cars. Cupcake offered to just loan her Jeeps out so we could split up and get everything taken care of. I asked Mongler if he wanted to see something cool and then “pressed a button” on Cupcake’s shoulder. She popped out both Jeeps from her arms and Mongler’s eyes got really big. That was worth it. Cheered me up for the moment too. Then Cupcake let the others take one Jeep to get their cars, and she drove Nightshade and I back to Dark Haven Bluffs.

                                I headed straight for the liquor cabinet while Karen and Chandra had a fit over Nightshade and Cupcake coming in. Tavia was a basket case even though she had already talked to Snowflake on the phone. I had already finished off a bottle of Grey Goose, gotten the Johnny Walker Double Black down, and found my suture kit by the time the others got home. I was just going to stitch myself up as best I could and drink the rest of the pain away. Snowflake and Nightshade keep enough booze in the house for me to get at least a little drunk and that’s what I was working on. Rose offered to help stitch me back together...and I could tell that she wanted desperately to help. I’ve never let anyone else stitch me up, but I figured what the hell. I asked Nightshade for her handcuffs, chained myself to the couch so I couldn’t jump too far, and keep the bottle with me. I was not drunk enough to let someone else stitch me up. I managed to not scream like a baby and just kept drinking. Nightshade actually kept bringing me water, which was not helping to dull the pain, but she was paying me attention so I made no complains. Keeping me hydrated was a good plan, I just wasn’t running on good plan mode. I called Sebastian to apologize for not showing up and said I got mugged. Then I went back to my room to finish the Johnny Walker I had. I went to sleep after a bottle of Jim Beam Knob Creek and one of Bombay Sapphire. Four bottles in about two hours I think. Yeah I’m gonna have to refill the bar this afternoon.

                                But now I gotta get a shower and go to work. Meditation, breakfast, and all that. Mmmm eggs.


                                Jasmine’s Diary

                                Tuesday, June 15, 2010, Night

                                It seems odd, having missed a day in my diary, mostly because I actually lost a day.

                                Yesterday afternoon we all gathered in the evening to do the Finding ritual to locate Shatina’s brother, Jamie. This time I managed to complete the ritual easily, and the small pendant around which I’d wrapped Jamie’s hair tugged me in the right direction quite insistently. We drove through the city, following the ritual until we arrived at an abandoned, run-down industrial park surrounded by a high chain-link fence. When we entered the area, we discovered that it was full of bookshelves that almost formed walls, and the shelves were stacked with countless books. And even though they were all slightly overgrown with thorny vines, the books and shelves did not show any signs of damage from exposure to the elements. It was terribly mysterious.

                                Then I noticed that the gate we’d entered through was no longer behind us. Instead, a maze of bookshelves stretched in all directions around us. Somehow we had wandered out of our world. The krewe panicked a bit, and I realized that I knew where we were. We were in the Hedge, a place Mongler has told me about. I tried to explain it to the others, but I really couldn’t explain it well until someone, Simon I think, made a comparison to the Underworld. Like the Underworld, the Hedge connects to our world but also serves as a gateway to deeper, darker realms beyond. And it is somewhere within, or maybe beyond the Hedge that the beings Mongler calls the Gentry live, which, as I think Gwen observed, makes them not unlike the Kerberoi.

                                In a panic, Alexia pulled out her cell phone to try and call someone, but there was no signal. Yet as soon as she opened it, a voice answered, and taunted Alexia by saying that it would find her! It was very disconcerting, especially for Alexia, who was put completely out of sorts by the experience.

                                We gathered our wits quickly enough, though. We didn’t have any provisions, so we couldn’t afford to wander around for too long, and we weren’t certain if it was even safe to eat or drink any food or water we might find in the Hedge. We also quickly determined, more by instinct than anything else, that it would be a bad idea to try and force our way through the walls of shelving and thorns. So solving the maze might be our only way out. Of course, I was a bit worried that solving the maze might just lead us into the clutches of the Gentry. It was obvious, at this point, that they already had Jamie, so he was sadly beyond our help.

                                Simon tried using Boneyard to have a look at the maze. The first attempt left him momentarily catatonic as his mind was stunned by the enormity and chaos of what he saw. But then he tried again! I feared he might lose his mind completely, but he was able to focus a bit better and got an idea of what lay around us, including a few spots we should avoid.

                                Among the things he saw were some things that looked like human-sized owls with the faces of spiders. They seemed to be tending to the books, and occasionally ate one or spun new ones from the silk that whispered in gossamer strand from their spider-like mouths.

                                Since the owl-spiders seemed like librarians, we decided we might ask them to help us find the way out…cautiously, of course, since we had no idea whether they would be hostile or not. We soon found some and though we couldn’t understand them, Doc managed to communicate by using his Primeval Marionette to attune to the ways of beasts. The owl-spiders did not know of any sure way out, but seemed to think there was an exit nearby if we continued down the path we had been traveling and took a left turn. They didn’t know exactly which left turn, though.

                                As Doc was relating all of this to us, he also observed that much like our local Underworld, this section of the Hedge was a dedicated library. Apparently that’s the effect New Haven has on these other realms. The owl-spiders told him that the books contained knowledge from dreams, but they were all written in languages we couldn’t understand.

                                We continued onward, looking for the exit, until we were ambushed by a car-sized spider-beast with owl’s eyes and a vicious, owl-like beak. It was accompanied by five of its much smaller offspring. They fell upon like a pack of ravenous wolves, and we were forced to fight for our lives.

                                Doc distracted the largest of the beasts while he summoned his powers, and it wounded him badly before he was able to lash out and kill it. Meanwhile, Alexia held her own against a couple of the beasts after manifesting her extra arms, and Gwen tore a couple of them apart with her Rage. Simon used the unpleasant powers of the Pit to trap one of the creatures in the ground. Natalie, however, was trying desperately to defend herself from spider-owl that leapt at her, so I ignored the one that rushed me to help save her. I drew the Primeval Caul and Cold-Wind Shroud about me and grabbed her attacker, and after several moments I froze its flesh and broke it in two. Meanwhile, the other was attacking me, but I maneuvered to avoid its blows, and my shroud absorbed any of its attacks that managed to hit, leaving me unscathed throughout the whole fight.

                                Eventually, we bested the creatures. Doc was a match for the biggest one, but the rest of us helped to take it down in the end because Doc was badly injured by it, and then Alexia and I finished off the rest. We patched up our wounds, and as we continued, I helped Doc along.

                                We eventually encountered a series of left turns, and began investigating them. Simon tried using Boneyard again, but the shock of what he couldn’t quite comprehend left him catatonic for several moments again, and we decided it was too risky to use Boneyard in the Hedge. So we agreed that I should scout ahead invisibly to see if I could find any sign of an exit before everyone else followed. Gwen wasn’t happy about me going off on my own. She was rightfully worried that the paths might change and leave me isolated, but I was willing to take the risk. Still, I didn’t stray too far from the others.

                                We passed several passages before we got to one that looked promising, including one path that led to trees bearing delicious-looking fruit, and another that led to a pool in which faceless nymphs were playing. The nymphs seemed like they were having a great deal of fun, and I almost felt tempted to join them, but I also had the feeling that the rest of the krewe would gladly jump in and start and orgy with them, which turned my stomach, so recommended we avoid that path. Yet another path led to a lighthouse the color of lavender and dusk that rose from the thorns like a monument and had a “Beware of...” sign in front. I can’t say exactly what I was to beware, because that part of the sign had been bitten away. It’s perhaps best that I never found out.

                                I kept noticing rather conspicuous piles of scattered books here and there along the paths. I had noticed them first just before we were attacked by the spider-owl, but I could not determine whether the other piles had any significance, though they might have been related to the creature we encountered a bit later. I should ask Mongler about it tomorrow when we meet to go see Cock Robin.

                                Eventually, we found a promising path, but as we passed by, books began tearing themselves from the shelves and vines and assembling into a hulking humanoid figure. We decided to flee it, but then Gwen heard something coming from the direction we were running. I ran ahead to find out what it was, and was relieved to see Mongler charging our way with a huge axe!

                                In the Hedge, apparently, his true appearance is obvious, so it was the first the others had seen him looking like a cross between an ogre and a dinosaur! He feared we had gone missing and had come to rescue us. With his help, Simon’s curses, and Alexia’s clever use of fire, we were able to defeat the book-creature. Had Mongler not been there, I fear we might not all have survived that encounter.

                                Mongler explained that he grew concerned when we didn’t show up to meet him and go see Cock Robin. I was confused, and thought he’d gotten the days mixed up, but it turns out we had been gone from the world for over a full day, and when he came for us, it was already Tuesday evening, not Monday! We’d only been in Hedge for four or five hours, so it was a huge shock to learn that we’d been missing for so much longer than that.

                                Mongler led us to the exit and out of the Hedge. He agrees that Jamie is lost to the Gentry now, and also believes that one of the Gentry wanted us to wander into its trap because it knew we were searching for Jamie. That’s a frightening prospect, as it means one of the Gentry has taken notice of us.

                                In any event, we’re all back safe and sound now. Chandra, Tavia, and Karen were out of their minds with worry. And I called Jean to apologize for missing our training session this morning. It was quite late, and I think I woke him, but I owed him an explanation. Not that I really had one prepared. I simply told him something came up and I couldn’t call. He must think I’m terrible.


                                Footnotes

                                Accent
                                I could tell the Gwen was panicking a bit after we entered the Hedge when I tried to explain where we were and she somehow heard “Hidge” when I said “Hedge.” She very aggressively asked me “What the hell is a Hidge!?” which confused me a bit until I realized she’d misinterpreted my Kiwi accent.

                                Not that my accent is completely Kiwi. I got most of it from my mum, but I had to study British English in school, and my Taiwanese accent creeps in as well, so I guess my English accent is quite confused…mostly Kiwi, but not quite. That’s what my cousins in New Zealand have told me, anyway.

                                Richard always told me it was completely enchanting. But Gwen didn’t seem to think so when we were in the Hedge. Fortunately Doc stepped in to interpret and help settle Gwen down a bit.

                                End Session XLIII

                                And that, good friends and true, is why you don’t enter the hedge.

                                One detail that was not mentioned by either party was when Doc was hurt Natalie gave him her one Capri-Sun drink she had with her…in theory a sign she cares given they did not know for how long they would be stuck. Anyway. The other note is Jasmine and Doc put the "beware of" sign in different places. It was outside the purple lighthouse. Oh, the "nymphs" Jasmine had to make a Resolve+Composure+Psyche roll to resist going in...her Virtue and Vice actually gave her a +2 and -2 which canceled each other out. Fortunately, Jasmine rocks at those rolls, and easily defeated the temptation to play with the nymphs.

                                As you can see through careful scouting and occasional Boneyard (I made Simon’s player make Resolve + Composure rolls when he used it) they avoided many of the perils, having to fight only the Book Giant (with help) and some Owlspiders. I’ll note the Owlspiders made for Combat number 4 and 5 in the campaign, which is a year and ¾ old now, and the first time we had two in a single session. Also, it seems like someone gets horribly wounded every time, first Alexia with the Ivory Claws, then Jasmine against the Rose Bride, then Doc vs. the Big Owlspider. I told Gwen’s player it’s her turn next.

                                But they managed to escape the Hedge without too much problem.

                                Check out our next session, where our heroes meet a very strange Darkling, Jasmine discoverers there is something in the humidifier, we jump ahead about a month in time, and discussion comes up about a very peculiar, very disturbing marionette/puppet show from 2/3 of the Krewe’s childhood. Comments and Questions welcome.
