                                    Anyway, Journals up in here

                                    Session XLIV

                                    Doc’s Log

                                    Doc’s Log: 1425 Hours, Wednesday, June 16, 2010

                                    Today hasn’t been too bad. Lifting stock was interesting but things were okay otherwise. I’ve been a little slower than usual, but that was just to keep from ripping out the stitches Rose put in me. While I had less free time thanks to moving slower, I did get some drawing in.

                                    Nightshade came over for lunch again today. She brought more Capri-Sun than usual today. I’m not sure if she expected me to still need hydrating or not. When I saw them, I remembered that she had given me one while we were in the Hedge. I know I was appreciative at the time, but I’m not sure I actually said anything to her about it. Nightshade is, due to a number of reasons, very focused on keeping herself out of harm’s way from outside forces. We had no way of knowing how long we might be stuck there and she gave me her only guaranteed source of drinkable fluid. I should have said something about it to her at the time. But I did notice so I brought up how thankful I was to have her around when I needed help. It was kinda surreal. We had one of those conversations back when I was still in the wheelchair. Hopefully she understood and it wasn’t too awkward.

                                    After that, I shifted the topic to the 27 Club. I hadn’t heard much about it lately so it’s cool to hear what’s been going on with that. They have reached the point of waiting to hear from other people before they can move forward with any of the properties. That sounded like a really good thing to me. It’s my own fault that I don’t have more to do with this endeavor but I’m so jealous of Karen for being able to share this with Nightshade. Still it’s good to hear about their progress so I know where they stand in case I can ever help.


                                    Doc’s Log: 1615 Hours, Wednesday, June 16, 2010

                                    Just got off the phone with Karen. We had another information exchange. Apparently baby Nightshade has a lot of pull on Nightshade already. Karen thinks she might spend lots of money on the kid. She said that baby Nightshade was very “sweet talkie” and already getting good at being manipulative as her mom. I did remind Karen that trying to interfere with how Nightshade handles her money is a very bad idea, for me more than others, but anyone in general. The overall concern is that Nightshade will be convinced that her little girl needs a car and Karen felt very strongly that Nightshade could not afford such an expense. Before I realized what I was saying, I made some comment about how it was like paying for college tuition. I’m not really sure where that came from but it was a low blow against someone that was honestly trying to help. Karen only paused for a second before continuing on like I hadn’t said anything. That’s probably for the best.

                                    It was somewhere in here that I had a new idea for images of a charm tattoo. A spectral or skeletal dragon head and wings rising up behind it with “Meddle Not In The Affairs of Dragons” emblazoned underneath it. It might have been coincidence or it might have been ironic timing. I know the rest of the Krewe would turn on Karen like a pack of wild dogs given the fact that baby Nightshade is her family and her responsibility, while Karen is neither. I have no intention of mentioning this fact to them, but the situation feels very Ancient Rome to me. I’ll keep my eyes open on the situation as much as I can, but I’m not sure Karen is going to like how this one turns out. I’m also not sure I care.

                                    Now it’s time to get ready to close up. We’ve got plans to meet Cock Robin tonight. I also need to check with Tavia to see how the portrait of Nightshade is coming.


                                    Doc’s Log: 2215 Hours, Wednesday, June, 16, 2010

                                    I should not be this tired so early at night. But it’s been an interesting evening.

                                    We went to Mongler’s office before heading to finally meet the mysterious Cock Robin. Mongler wanted to have a serious moment with us before we went. He needed for us to know that Cock Robin liked to touch for no known reason. I think his depictions of previous violence against Cock Robin shocked at least Rose. We should keep in mind at all times that Cock Robin was dangerous honestly insane and very powerful in dreams. We were not to hurt or kill it. If “he” reached for anything inappropriate Mongler promised to break its arm. We didn’t need to leave any residue from powers or it might get interested in us. This being was crazy even for Changelings, which really didn’t mean anything to any of us. I took it to mean that in all ways I should handle it as though it were a mental patient. I was okay with that, but I’m not sure all the others were on the same page as I was.

                                    We gathered up and head out. We went to a place that I’m not sure I even knew was there, which made me a little uneasy after our random trip to the Hedge. The building was made of corrugated metal and I spotted holes in the roof. There were four different private property signs around the building, just from what I could see from the car. Mongler led the way by beating on the door and announcing our arrival before going in.

                                    The being calling itself Cock Robin was a short, pale thing with a huge curved nose. It acted like a Skeksi from the Dark Crystal. We told him what information we had about Shatina and the Hedge while Mongler stood to keep us separate from it. Ronin did most of the talking, which might not have been the best option, but she spoke up first so it directed its answered to her. At one point, it left to get a book. Nightshade made a Gelfing joke and we all agreed as to just how broke this thing was. When it came back, we got to the very surreal moment when it announced to us as much as to the air that “Fetches always have something wrong.” I glanced at Mongler to see if something was happening we needed to be aware of, but he seemed intent on bringing Cock Robin back to task. It let us know that it kept its fetch downstairs. This led to an uncomfortable discussion about Mongler’s fetch, which is apparently still around, but totally off limits to Cock Robin. Ultimately we left Cock Robin to its crazy with the understanding we needed to get “a piece” of Shatina for it to study. We decided that we would get a brush, a mirror, and at least temporarily her phone. That would give us hair to give to Cock Robin, a way for Snowflake to keep watch on her, and information for Ronin to do her detective thing.

                                    I think Ronin, Rose, and Snowflake are going out tonight to do that. I’m going to stay home with my dog and try not to hurt. I’m not really able to help much in this area anyway.


                                    Doc’s Log: 1930 Hours, Thursday, June 17, 2010

                                    Today was blissfully uneventful.

                                    Lunch with Nightshade was relaxing as always.

                                    During dinner, we had the Krewe news session like always. The Magic Thieves had some excitement last night. Rose is the best at getting in and around places unseen so she went in with Rose ready to bolt and Snowflake watching the whole place like an all-seeing eye. Not that we really needed much more evidence, but given what Rose dealt with, we’re pretty sure not only is Shatina a fetch, but also that what Mongler calls the Gentry are still keeping watch on her. The short version is that most of the room gave Rose the feeling something was there watching her and a humidifier seemed to be making some strange mist shapes in the pink night light. Rose looked inside but when she put the lid back on, she realized that she had no idea what she’d just seen. Snowflake apparently had the same reaction from his vantage point and he could see through illusions. This led to Rose throwing the lid off and even though she was invisible this negative copy of her reached from the water and tried to grab her. I didn’t understand all the details but something about the room changed after that, since she came back to return Shatina’s phone. This is bad juju.

                                    On a happier note, Ronin let us know that she had successfully invested two of the gold bars this morning, and the funds from this were going into an offshore account to which we would each receive a debit card. The original total before we start earning the interest is just about $2.5 million! Hurray Gold! I have no idea what 20% interest is on that much money, but I don’t think we’ll need to worry about expenses if we ever need to travel. I can foresee the medical station and our alchemy lab going live sooner rather than later now. Holy shit…I don’t even know what else to say.

                                    On a related note, we’re leaving tomorrow for the Flesh Fair in New Orleans. I don’t think I’ll take my journal as I don’t know if I’ll have time to write in it and I damn sure don’t want anyone to go through it.


                                    Doc’s Log: 0210 Hours, Tuesday, June 22, 2010

                                    I’m about to drop but I wanted to get this down first. The Flesh Fair and New Orleans was wild, as per. Other than just fun stuff, I mingled with some of the other medic types at the Fair. I barely remember names and have no time for nicknames, so I’ll just summarize. In general, Sin-Eaters either don’t help and/or don’t talk about other supernatural creatures. What I did get a few people to admit follows:

                                    Vampires are hard to patch up. (I knew this already)
                                    If a werewolf needs help healing, dig a hole.
                                    Changelings are a little too out there for anyone to admit to knowing.

                                    If there’s anything else with a name running around, no one was offering up information.

                                    Now I’m going to bed. Tomorrow I need to take these stitches out.


                                    Jasmine’s Diary


                                    Wednesday, June 16, 2010, Afternoon

                                    I had a chat with Jean this morning about all the strange things that have happened with me lately. I could tell he was concerned. After all, first I couldn’t meet for a week because I’d been badly burned, and then I disappeared entirely for a day. But he never asked if anything strange was going on…not really. I guess Gwen is right, and Jean and I aren’t going to work out. Not that I’ve thought we would for some time, really. I guess I’m not really approachable in the right way for him.

                                    That said, I tried to help him understand. I explained my recent injury and disappearance by telling him the truth, more-or-less. I told him that I and my house mates are part-time paranormal investigators, and that we sometimes run into odd or dangerous things. He was not as surprised as I thought he might be. In fact, he seemed to accept it fairly readily. Mostly, he was just worried about what other dangers that line of work might present. He does care about me, after all.


                                    Wednesday, June 16, 2010, Evening

                                    We visited Cock Robin today to see if he could help us learn more about Shatina and whether or not she is a fetch. Mongler gave us plenty of warnings about Cock Robin before going to see him, enough to give us the impression that he could be quite dangerous under the right circumstances. But Mongler was with us, and apparently bullies his smaller associate to keep him in line, so I really felt more sorry for Cock Robin than anxious about anything he might try. I assume that Mongler knew what he was doing, but still, his threats seemed a bit excessive.

                                    Cock Robin was a scrawny changeling who resembled a cross between a person and a featherless baby bird. He was quite grotesque, actually, and I don’t think I’ve every seen anyone who look so obviously mad. But he seemed quite a lot about fetches.

                                    He believes, from our description, that Shatina is a fetch, but we are going to fetch some bits of hair or fingernail clippings from her so that he can test them and find out more. If Shatina is a fetch, then Shatina is likely trapped beyond the Hedge with one of the gentry. And as for Jamie, his fetch may have failed, which would explain the set of left-over clothes Shatina found. So, tonight, Gwen, Simon and I are going to go collect something of Shatina’s that Cock Robin can use to tell if she’s really a fetch or not.

                                    I should also note that Gwen informed us tonight that she’s managed to convert a couple of our gold bars into cash. She’s set up a krewe account and gave us all debit cards. Apparently we have well over $2 million in the account! I don’t really have any use for that much money, and I hope the others don’t fritter it all away on extravagant things.


                                    Wednesday, June 16, 2010, Late Night

                                    Gwen, Simon and I secretly visited Shatina’s home tonight, and I crept in to find evidence that she really is a fetch. Simon remained in the car with Gwen and kept watch with Boneyard in case anything went wrong. Meanwhile, I unlocked the Caul of Stillness and crept into the house as living shadow.

                                    I managed to gather one of her hairbrushes and a few fingernail clippings, which should be plenty for Cock Robin to perform whatever experiments or rituals he uses to learn more. and I took her phone to Gwen, who copied its data just in case. I also found an old pocket watch. It was not working and had a cracked face, but it must have been in Shatina’s family for awhile and it resonated with death. I thought it would make a nice charm for Simon, so I stole it. I retrospect, I think it was a horrible thing to do, but Shatina isn’t really Shatina, is she? It will do more good in our hands than buried forgotten in one of her vanity drawers.

                                    As I searched Shatina’s room, I couldn’t shake the feeling that there was definitely something wrong there. Shatina was sleeping peacefully, but I couldn’t shake the feeling that I was being watched. Also, there was a humidifier that produced an odd, swirling mist that seemed to make strange patterns in the pinkish glow produced by Shatina’s night light. I reinforced my stealth by unlocking the Stillness Shroud to become invisible shadow, and then lifted the lid off the humidifier to have peek inside. But I immediately replaced it with no memory of what I had seen. I later learned that Simon had a similar experience watching with Boneyard.

                                    Disturbed by the odd sensation of missing a moment of my life, I decided to brave another look and this time tossed the lid aside so I couldn’t immediately replace it. And when I did, I saw my own vague reflection in the water inside the humidifier (even though I was invisible), and it reached out to grab me with long, spectral arms! I managed to dodge away in time, and then the thing in the humidifier disappeared.

                                    I decided to leave well-enough alone for now. I didn’t know what I was dealing with, though perhaps I should have removed the humidifier altogether. Instead, I replaced the lid so nothing would seem amiss when Shatina awoke. It was then that I noticed the door to the room had closed, even though I know I left it open. I was eager to leave then, and phased through the wall to deliver the evidence and phone to Gwen. But when I returned a few minutes later to replace the phone so Shatina wouldn’t miss it, the door was open again. It was quite spooky, and none of us, even Simon, know what we’re up against.

                                    I’ll make sure to get the evidence to Mongler tomorrow so he can pass it along to Robin.


                                    [Excerpts from Jasmine’s Diary Entries, June 17 to July 5, 2010]

                                    Saturday, June 19, 2010, Late Night

                                    We have arrived and enjoyed our first day at the flesh fair in New Orleans. I’d never been to New Orleans before. It seems like a fascinating city, though perhaps a bit boisterous for my tastes. The flesh fair itself has been terribly interesting, though I’m not really sure what to make of it. I’ve never met so many other sin-eaters before. And they are all so different, so unique. It’s easy, when one deals with supernatural things as we do, to begin stereotyping. But really, we’re all as different and unique as normal people. I mean, Natalie and I are almost as different as can be, and yet we’re both Silent.

                                    Honestly, the fair has been a bit overwhelming. Mostly I’ve just stuck with Gwen, not really knowing what else to do with myself. I was hoping to trade for some charms, but they are in high demand and I really didn’t have anything to trade for them. So I’ve mostly been staying quiet and out of the way, except when Gwen tries to shove me into the spotlight. She means well, but it’s been a little uncomfortable.

                                    Visiting some of the jazz clubs has been wonderful, though. Gwen doesn’t really care for it, so I’ve had to slip away on my own. There’s something so cozy and romantic about listening to the live music in a city known for its jazz. I found this really nice place that has a sort of 60’s era jazz feel. It’s a little…”beatniky,” but aside from that I felt perfectly at home there. Well, as much as I feel at home anywhere. Anyway, it made me think of Audrey Hepburn’s film Funny Face, which is one of my favorites.

                                    Doc and Natalie have stuck together throughout our trip so far, and it’s nice to see her paying so much attention to him. I suppose I’ve been a bit unfair toward Natalie over the past weeks. She really is trying to make her relationship with Doc work, even if she does still have a lot of challenges to overcome. Natalie and I don’t really communicate well with each other, but I can still see some of the positive changes she’s been making in her life. I don’t know if it’s Doc or the way she’s reconnecting with her daughter, but it seems like she’s doing less drugs, and I don’t think she’s been sleeping around any now that she’s formalized her relationship with Doc and Karen. I still think that three-way relationship is a bad idea and that Karen’s a crutch who is going to make it harder for Natalie to overcome some of her issues in the long run, but at least she’s giving it all her best. I just hope the trend continues.


                                    Wednesday, June 3, 2010, Evening

                                    I met with Mongler today, and he gave me what information Cock Robin was able to learn about Shatina’s fetch. There’s no longer any doubt that she is a fetch, for one thing. Also, she doesn’t know what she is. She thinks she really is Shatina. But if she awakens to what she really is, either on her own or when prompted by her creator, she will gain access to a host of supernatural powers that will make her quite dangerous indeed. Unfortunately, there’s not much else we can do right now other than keep an eye on her and make sure we’re ready to act if she shows any signs of awakening. It might be a good idea to eliminate the fetch immediately, but Mongler seems to think waiting and watching will be best, and he knows far more about these matters than I do.


                                    Thursday, June 24, 2010, Late Night

                                    It seems that my house mates are trying to expand the range of their supernatural abilities. Simon asked me to help him almost drown so that he can learn how to manipulate the Tear-Stained Key. That key is one of my specialities, even though I don’t really use it as much as the Stillness or Primeval keys these days. Plus, I’m a swimmer with extensive life guard training and experience, so who better to keep him from really killing himself? Fortunately, things went well, and though the experience was unpleasant for both of us, Simon came through all right.

                                    Meanwhile, Gwen decided to attune herself to Pyre-Flame by getting struck my lightning. Honestly, she’s so flamboyant! Why couldn’t she do it safely by burning herself a bit like Alexis did? Anyway, Gwen found her way to the roof during a thunderstorm tonight and managed to the be hit. Fortunately, Doc was on site to make sure she was okay, but I still think it was foolish and I let her know as much!


                                    Monday, June 30, 2010, Late Night

                                    Julian came for dinner tonight! I helped Alexia prepare dinner and cooked ba-wan dumplings, which are one of my favorite Taiwanese dishes. Julian seemed to like the ba-wan, which made me happy. He was just as charming as I remember him being at the party, and he chatted about his graduate studies and told us anecdotes from his past with Simon, all of which were both entertaining and endearing. I wanted to ask Julian to walk with me in the garden cemetery after dinner, but thought it might be too forward of me, so I contented myself with enjoying his company with the rest of the krewe over dinner and then afterward, in the parlour. Still, the others did manage to conspire to leave the two of us alone to chat for a bit after dinner. It was enchanting! The Bride certainly agrees, but her thoughts very much mirror my own these days, so I was hardly surprised. She says Julian reminds her of her lost love, but I think perhaps that she is projecting a bit.

                                    In any event, it was a good evening.


                                    Friday, July 2, 2010, Noon

                                    Since the krewe has a bit of a lull in its activities right now, I’ve decided to go back to Taiwan to visit my father. It’s been almost a year since I’ve been home, and he and I weren’t exactly happy with each other last time I left for America. He still wants me to stay in Taiwan, and I simply cannot do that. But I miss him, and want to see him. And I know he misses me terribly. It’s hard for him, with mum dead and his daughter living halfway around the world. It’s been unkind of me to go so long without visiting.

                                    My flight leaves on Monday, and I’m planning on staying about two weeks. It will be nice to be back in Taipei, though I don’t think it will feel like home anymore.

                                    Footnotes

                                    Julian
                                    I asked Simon a bit about Julian, and was relieved to learn that Julian already knows quite a bit about the occult, and even about sin-eaters. Apparently Julian has been a friend of Simon’s for a long time, and has always known that Simon’s family dabbled in the occult. Julian often helped Simon with his arcane pursuits, and after Simon became a sin-eater, before we found him, Julian helped Simon experiment with his new powers.

                                    I can’t express how relieved I was to hear that Julian has already been initiated into the world of the supernatural. As I’m learning with Jean, becoming involved with someone who doesn’t know what he’s getting into is…challenging. Not that I’m really involved with Julian….


                                    Fetches

                                    Cock Robin explained how fetches are made. Each one is built on a “piece” of a person, like her shadow, reflection, regrets, dreams, or practically anything else you could imagine. Other things are mixed in to give the fetch form, and once it is enchanted, even changelings can’t tell what it is on sight.


                                    American Culture and Idioms

                                    The Dark Crystal

                                    Natalie made a creepy comment while we were interacting with Cock Robin. Something like “Smells like gelfling.” As usual, I had no idea what she was talking about, but Gwen later explained that Cock Robin reminded them of creatures called “skeksis” from a Jim Henson film called The Dark Crystal. We watched the movie later, and Cock Robin really was a lot like the skeksi characters. Though it wasn’t actually a skeksi that said “Smells like gelfling.”

                                    I enjoyed the film. It was quite magical, though a bit odd and scary. I was surprised by how convincing and realistic the puppetry was!


                                    Evil Twins

                                    Gwen was quite agitated when Simon and I told her what had happened with the humidifier in Shatina’s room. She apparently feared that if the thing in the humidifier had gotten me, then my “evil twin” might show up, which I suppose is reasonable, given Shatina’s situation. That said, I was completely confused when Gwen started going on about goatees. Eventually she managed to explain that in the Star Trek show, evil twins had goatees or dressed strangely, and that they were the opposite of the person they were based on. I pointed out that if that were so, my evil twin would be delightfully happy and probably insatiably randy, and therefore instantly recognizable, which seemed to amuse Gwen enough to calm her worries. Of course, if a double did show up, it probably wouldn’t be so obvious. Shatina’s fetch certainly seems like the real thing.
                                    She did become quite cross with me when I got Star Wars and Star Trek mixed up, though.
                                    Honestly, I think she watches too much TV.


                                    Drowning

                                    I tried not let it show, but helping Simon almost drown to learn the Tear-Stained key was quite painful and traumatic for me. I kept remembering how I died, paralyzed and floating beneath the surface of the pool, unable to even struggle against the inevitable as my lungs filled with water and I realized that I was going to lose not only my life, but my love as well. Returning to that dark memory was almost more than I could bear, even if it is what defined me as a sin-eater.


                                    Natalie’s Tweets

                                    Wednesday, June 16th

                                    -Barely up in time for lunch with Doc. Yesterday completely screwed my sleep schedule.

                                    -Lunch with Doc!

                                    -Doc is still drawing stuff. I am still a delicious Strawberry deathcake.

                                    -Picking up booze with Karen and Simon, woo!

                                    -That’s only two underage people. Technically.

                                    -Gonna pass on the George Dickel.

                                    -Karen with a bottle of wine in her hand…mmmm…what were we talking about?

                                    -I am such a perv today.

                                    -I am given to understand that at least for purposes of an upcoming meeting that the bird is, in fact, the word.

                                    -Yea, I feel bad just typing that.

                                    End Session XLIV

                                    Most events were pretty well covered in the journals to a point.

                                    I was terribly proud of the scene with the humidifier. The room got pretty quiet and the PCs were less...cocky...in their approach with it, but the Shatina storyline is one of the few that really has seemed to rattle them IC. They were pretty...careful, which was cool.

                                    As noted a couple new keys, though they have not picked them up yet. Alexia also picked up Marionette, and Natalie maxed Boneyard.

                                    The last 45 minutes or so of the game covered Tavia's drawing the TV show Candle Cove to the PCs attention. Mainly Gwen, at first. After some research they all had a discussion; Natalie (and Jasmine, though she was retconned out of the scene) did not remember not being locals, but Simon, Gwen, Doc, and Alexia, being locals as kids, did. The show involved marionettes and puppets, as well as one live girl and omnipresent calliope music. The more people remembers (with Int+Composure rolls) the more sinister the show seemed to be, with little things they missed as kids and some things they understood all too well. Some even remembered a "final episode" with screaming and crying that they felt compelled to turn off. They resolved to investigate it, and that investigation was the focus of last night's session. More details will, no doubt, be forthcoming then.

                                    Jasmine and Gwen's players had to miss last nights session. As such, it was assumed Jasmine was still in Taiwan (hence the ending of her journal implying she would be, and retonning her from the show discussion) and Gwen was otherwise occupied. There will probably be a short Jasmine journal covering events in Taiwan according to her player.

                                    I'll see you then.

                                        Hello folks, I hope this message finds you well.

                                        Good news and bad news. The good news is Doc and Jasmine journals. Bad news is neither actually covers the session 2 weeks ago. Doc's entirely covers the downtime during the session a month ago, and Jasmine's covers her trip to Taiwan. This is partially because both Jasmine and Gwen's players had to miss two weeks ago due to illnesses, last enough minute we were unable to reschedule (not the players faults at all, of course!). So Doc's journal is actually covering the timeframe covered by the end of Jasmine's last journal, not anything in her current journal. It's out of sync, but you know. Jasmine's journal does cover stuff that came up in the game tonight, however. Long as that's clear, lets roll.

                                        Session XLV

                                        Doc's Log

                                        Doc’s Log: 1955 Hours, Friday, June 25, 2010

                                        I’d feel like I should say it’s been a crazy week, but we haven’t done much. Not as a Krewe anyway. I have been trying like crazy to make sure everything in the shop is perfect in case I get one of the “surprise” inspectors to check out my new tattoo area, before the state approves my permit.

                                        I said we haven’t done much, but that’s not to say nothing happened since we got back from New Orleans. Ronin has been looking into learning a new Key. Because I’ve spent a lot of time in the Library lately, I’ve been around while she looked up things on Pyre-Flame. She really wasn’t thrilled with sticking her hand in an oven or something like that. Wednesday, we were supposed to have a thunderstorm. Now I’m not completely happy about this, but she decided to go up on the roof and do the Lambada with one of the lightning rods. She did at least have the good sense to tell me she was going to do it first, so I could be there in case something went wrong though. I didn’t enjoy sitting on the roof during a downpour, but I wasn’t about to let her get struck so her heart could stop or something dumb like that. We face enough ways to die without the random stuff making life harder.

                                        While she was prancing around, she slipped. I lunged for her to keep her from falling off the roof. I don’t know if she put on extra jewelry or found some metal bracers or what, but as soon as I grabbed for her, this huge flash hit and knocked her back into the roof. Unfortunately, it also arced over into me and blasted me backwards too. I was blinded and having a hard time moving when I realized Ronin was laughing to my right. I reached over until I could feel what I hoped was her shoulder and dragged her to me. She made some joke about buying her dinner first before I kicked her in the shin. I would have felt bad about it later until she belted me in the solar plexis. After I could breathe again, I just wanted to go inside. I didn’t tell Ronin that I was hit too as the rest of the Krewe were trying to get us off the roof. We didn’t need anyone blaming her for something that might have happened to me.

                                        You speak as though your presence was by accident and yet you know you aided The Angel’s vessel in finding her knowledge. The level of humility you strive for is confusing. Seeking undue accolades holds no honor, but hiding from honor that is yours serves no purpose.

                                        He keeps me straight even when I don’t want it I guess. But then that might be why we get along like we do.

                                        The other reason I’m writing this is because Rose got some news back from Cock Robin about our tests the other night and I haven’t written it down yet. The person we recognize as Shatina is in fact a Fetch. She apparently has the power to trap the real Shatina (who would now be a Changeling of some kind) in a mirror. By all accounts, Cock Robin found she has used no supernatural powers to date. The likelihood is that she has no idea she is a Fetch. This both is relieving and makes things more complicated.

                                        We talked about Manchurian Candidates a few times while I was still at Camp Lejune. Ignoring the macho bullshit, no one was really sure what to think about it. It was a scary thought that someone we knew and trusted could just be “activated” one day and become something else. This is the most logical comparison I can draw to Shatina’s situation.

                                        At least we were able to give Mongler a heads up that someone might be coming out of the Hedge and will need help. Not that we have a clue where, when, or if.


                                        Doc’s Log: 2015 Hours, Monday, June 28, 2010

                                        We had a guest for dinner tonight. Julian dropped by. I’m not sure if Snowflake set it up or if he just came to see Rose, but she was very adorable and excited. She helped Cupcake in the kitchen and surprised us all with another Taiwanese dish. Ba-wan dumplings aren’t something I was familiar with but Rose thought they were authentic; Cupcake made them delicious and our special guest seemed impressed.

                                        I’m not sure just how serious this might turn out, but Rose seems very eager to find out, in as much as she’s eager for anything. This could definitely go somewhere. I just wish Snowflake would let me know before he does stuff like this. Tavia doesn’t need help getting doe-eyed.


                                        Doc’s Log: 0415 Hours, Tuesday, June 29, 2010

                                        I don’t even know exactly how to write this. I’m not in the habit of documenting my sex life. But something has changed in Nightshade. I’m not going to get into graphic details, but what she’s after seems to have changed. I’m not sure how to take this. Does she want to freak me out or is she thinking (directly) about having another baby?

                                        I don’t have the brain power to go into it all now, but my mind is blown.


                                        Doc’s Log: 2015 Hours, Friday, July 2, 2010

                                        Being that it’s Practice Night, I had Rose teach me the ceremony to dedicate charms. I’ve been reading up on them in the library for a while now. I’m pretty sure I can make tattoos into charms if I have the right materials. I’ll need to be really careful with those materials though. Not the kind of stuff I keep with the rest of my tattooing supplies ever.

                                        So now my drawing is more important than just getting an image out of my head. It can be a way for us to really move in Sin-Eater circles. Some day. First I have to get everything together and get some work visible.

                                        Gotta get back to this drawing thing. Art is calling!

                                        Yeah that sounded just as lame in my head. I was hoping on paper would look better. Nope.


                                        Doc’s Log: 2115 Hours, Monday, July 5, 2010

                                        I’ve been spending a lot of time in the Library while we’re taking it easy and learning a little more about supernatural politics and drawing whenever I get the chance. Sebastian has been working with me again and I think I’m really getting the hang of a few other dances.

                                        Rose flew back to Taiwan this morning. She’s been talking with her Father which is new. Apparently they weren’t on great terms before so she’s going home to spend some time with him and just visit in general. Two weeks without Rose around will definitely feel different.

                                        After we dropped her off at the airport, everyone just kinda drifted into different directions in a daze. I managed to find Nightshade before Karen made off with her. I really needed to know what exactly she wanted. The change in her actions had not only continued, but intensified. And I didn’t know any way to ask other than just straight to the point. Her response was possibly the most haunting thing I’ve ever heard. She told me that she wanted a lot of things. Just seeing the words doesn’t really tell the whole story. She was so sad when she said it, like everything she wanted was out of her reach. I knew that yes she did want another child, apparently with me, and I’m not sure how that will work. But there was so much more meaning in those few words. I don’t know everything she meant but I knew she was feeling unfulfilled and not in a sexual way. It was heartbreaking. I really don’t like the idea, but I think I’m going to have to talk to Karen to see is she understands this anymore than I do.


                                        Doc’s Log: 1930 Hours, Saturday, July 10, 2010

                                        So no more information on what’s going on with Nightshade and I haven’t been able to talk to Karen privately yet either. But I did get a surprise when Nightshade got up for lunch today. She announced that we were going to look at the possible sites for the 27 Club. It’s Socializing day so that kinda fit. Karen tagged along and the three of us drove around looking at multiple places around town. A few were available for us to look in, probably because they have been around so much, but I didn’t complain. It was nice to finally get involved in this process. I thought I wanted to do this with just Nightshade but Karen was actually very helpful in showing me some things I didn’t notice or explaining things I didn’t already get. I took notes at each place and when we got back to the house, we discussed what I thought of each place. There were a few concerns I had with one or two locations and I’m not sure that my input mattered but Nightshade discussed them like they weren’t really in the running anymore. I finally felt like I was becoming a part of this piece of Nightshade’s life.

                                        Now we’re going to hook back up with Snowflake and go clubbing I think.


                                        Jasmine’s Diary

                                        Monday, July 5, 2010, In Flight

                                        Gwen drove me to the airport this morning, and my flight to Taiwan has been uneventful so far, except that the young American man in the seat beside me kept yacking at me for the first two hours. I really wasn’t interested because he seemed to be flirting with me and trying to practice his terrible Chinese. The worst part was that he was trying to speak Cantonese, which is the wrong dialect for Taiwan. As a result, I couldn’t understand half of what he was saying, which led him to conclude that I wasn’t really from Taiwan either, and that my Chinese was worse than his! Still, my coldness must have put him off, because he eventually gave up and stopped trying to chat. I think that when I started writing in fluent Chinese in my diary, he realized what a fool he had been and finally shut up.

                                        There’s still several hours until I land in Taipei, and the time zone is nearly opposite Connecticut, so I’m going to get some sleep to help mitigate the jet lag.


                                        Tuesday, July 6, 2010, Night

                                        It’s been a good day. My father was at the airport to pick me up, and when we saw each other, it was like all the bitterness over my decision to stay in America evaporated. I didn’t think coming back to Taiwan would feel like coming home again, but with my father there, welcoming me with open arms, it really did.

                                        Even though I was worn out from my flight, we decided to stop by one of my favorite restaurants on the way to my father’s house, and had a nice conversation over dinner where we caught up a bit. Getting real bubble tea was delightful! Then, once we returned home, I showed him pictures of people back in New Haven. He said that he’d worried that Richard’s death had changed me, and that talking to me now and seeing the pictures of my friends, he can see that it did change me. But it wasn’t a bad change. And now, he can see that while I’ve changed, I’m also still the same little girl that he raised.

                                        We talked about that for awhile. We needed to. He wasn’t really surprised that I live in creepy old house with a bunch of odd house-mates. I’ve always been interested in the occult and something of an outsider, so when he stopped to think about it, it only seemed natural. Honestly, he said he was just glad I had friends now. And as for how I’ve been changed by Richard’s death, well, that’s only natural too. He said that in retrospect, he’d be more worried about me if I hadn’t been changed by it. After all, it’s the nature of existence to flow and shift.

                                        I want to tell my father what really happened to me when Richard died, but I don’t know if he’s ready for that now.


                                        Sunday, July 11, 2010, Night

                                        I’ve spent much of the last week spending time with my father, training in tai chi, helping out around my father’s antique shop, and visiting family and places I’ve missed since I’ve been away. The family visits are largely a matter of function. About half my extended family dislikes me. The stigma of my paranormal abilities still clings to me, so they don’t really want too much to do with me anyway. Ironically, I think they’re also displeased that I’ve decided to live in America instead of Taiwan. The other half think I’m grand because of my Olympic career, but they don’t really have much to do with me because I was away so often even before I went to America. At least some of my younger second-cousins think its “cool” that I live in America. I don’t think I’ve ever been described as “cool” before.

                                        I’ve noticed a few antiques around my father’s shop that carry the residue of death. I’ve asked him about them, but he didn’t really know the history of most. He was curious why I was asking, and I told him, as honestly as I could, that I still have a sixth sense for things, and those items in particular seemed to resonate with me. Still, I think he knows I’m not telling him everything. I could never read objects like that when I was a girl, and if I could, he would know about it since I was raised around the store and never really kept my abilities from him. I kept them from my mum, because she didn’t really understand, but not from my father.

                                        Maybe I’ll convince my father to let me have one of the items before I head back to America.


                                        Monday, July 12, 2010, Night

                                        I got a little bored today, and decided to have a look around the neighborhoods surrounding my father’s house to see if I couldn’t locate any Avernian gates or haunts. I made sure to check places where I knew people had died, particularly people whose deaths I had foreseen. I found both a haunt and a gate in a small, abandoned Buddhist shrine some distance from the antique shop. I remember that the lone priest who maintained the shrine hung humself for mysterious reasons, reasons that remain unsolved as far as I know. It happened when I was a child, and I had a horrible vision of the priest’s lingering death some days before he killed himself. Afterward, a rumor started in the neighborhood that I caused his death somehow because of my vision. It was one instance where my gift was interpreted as a curse by those around me, and so I stopped sharing my visions after that.


                                        Wednesday, July 14, 2010, Afternoon

                                        I’ve just learned that my uncle Guang Sung has disappeared, apparently from his home. He’s been missing at least a week now, probably more, but since he’s sort of a loner and an outsider among the family, no one really noticed at first.

                                        The police don’t really have any leads, so his disappearance is a complete mystery. Hearing about it sparked a lot of memories about Uncle Guang. I haven’t really thought about him for years, but when I was just a girl, he was quite interested in me because of my supernatural gift. He was a student of the occult, though I don’t think he has any supernatural gift of his own.

                                        I decided to look into the disappearance, so I found out where he lived and went to investigate. I didn’t find any leads either, but I can say with certainty that he was performing a ritual of some sort just before he vanished. Most of the ritual implements and many of his other occult books and supplies had been removed by the time I was able to look about, so I can’t say what he was trying to do, but I suspect that something supernatural is behind his disappearance. Also, I sensed the residue left when someone died in the place, yet it wasn’t quite right. I had the distinct feeling that no one had actually died there, just that the residue of death draped the place as though someone had. It was a very odd feeling, and made me worry for my uncle.

                                        I’m going to see what else I can dig up before my flight back to Connecticut.


                                        Sunday, July 18, 2010, Late Night

                                        My father treated me to a bit of private party with a few of our closest relatives before I go back to America tomorrow. It was fun, though of course I don’t really get along well with such festive occasions. Still, I put on my best face for dad. He gave me a gift during the party. It was one of the items I asked about in the shop: a century-old string of jade Buddhist prayer beads. He had a few stories about its history, but he doesn’t know which is actually true. It resonates with the Primeval Key, so I look forward to turning it into a charm and treasuring it as a token of my father’s affection.

                                        Sadly, I haven’t learned anything else about my uncle’s disappearance. It frustrates me to leave without being able to help him, but I really have no choice. Maybe the krewe can arrange a visit to Taiwan soon to follow up on thing. I could certainly use their help. I don’t know if my uncle is alive or dead, but either way, I feel I should do what I can for him.

                                        I’d best get some sleep. My flight leave quite early tomorrow morning.


                                        Monday, July 19, 2010, Morning

                                        I’m on my flight back to Connecticut. I’m sad to leave my father, but I need to return to the krewe. Still, I’m glad I had such a pleasant visit. It was not without its frustrations, but all in all, it was good to be home.
                                        Though it wasn’t really home anymore, was it? I mean, Taiwan will always be my home, but it isn’t where I belong anymore. Reflecting on that saddens me, but like my father said, things change. People change. And that’s the way it should be. Meditating on that makes me worry that parts of me aren’t changing like they should. But it also gives me more hope for others, especially Natalie, who is changing, I think. Funny how absence helps me see the good in things.

                                        Footnotes

                                        My Father

                                        My father dressed up to come meet me at the airport, like it was a very special occasion (he looked quite handsome for a fifty-year-old man!) For him, I think meeting me in person after almost a year without seeing one another really was a very special occasion. He loves me a lot, and I think he wanted to put aside the angry words we shared when we last parted. We’ve talked since then, of course, but we had to meet in person to sweep those bad feelings aside.

                                        My father has always been a pillar of strength for me. I was distressed when my mum died and I sensed his strength waver. But no matter what, he has always supported and encouraged me. He knew I was special even before I started to foresee death. And he’s always encouraged me to treasure that gift and ignore the accusations of those who don’t understand it.

                                        I owe a lot of who I am to my father. He taught me all I know about Buddhism and Taoism. And he taught me most of my tai chi and martial arts skills. He’s a very capable martial artist too, and teaches a tai chi class in his off time. He always pushed me in my swimming career, never once letting me become discouraged and attending every swim meet, competition, and event.


                                        My Father’s Antique Shop

                                        By trade, my father runs a large antique shop, and his home is on the second story above it. My mum had an interest in antiques, which is how they met. She found his shop on one of her business trips to Taiwan while looking for some authentic Chinese additions to her collection, and the two of them just hit it off. I used to help out in the antiques shop a little when I was younger, and I think it contributed to my occult education. You would be surprised how many odd items come through the doors.


                                        Uncle Guang

                                        Uncle Guang was an awkward loner who didn’t have much interest in other people, but he was always interested in me when I was a girl, and went out of his way to talk to me. Eventually, the family stepped in and I never saw him again. I didn’t understand at the time, but I know now that they thought he was molesting me. Even though there was no real evidence of it, they still thought he was trying to take advantage of me somehow, and so they separated us.

                                        But he wasn’t molesting me. He never touched me, and all he really wanted to talk about was my gift. He was the only one in my family who wanted to know more about it. Most feared it. But he wanted to understand it, to know more about my experiences. His interest was very academic, and even though he was kind to me, I don’t think he was all that interested in me as a person, really. That said, it was sometimes very scary talking about what I saw when I had my visions, and I remember that he always tried to be comforting. I don’t know whether that was because he really cared about my feelings or because he felt that it would help me talk more freely about my gift, but at the time, I was just happy that someone other than my father was being supportive of my abilities.

                                        End Session XLV

                                        OK that should give some more downtime info. Again, for Jasmine's perspectives some of what Doc mentioned, see the last journals.

                                        Natalie's melancholy is complicated. Her reconnection to her daughter has given her some desire to have a child she won't "fail" though she is a sensible person (sorta) and is not doing anything risky. She sees it as difficult for her relationship with Karen if it happened, so that also keeps her from it. At the same time, her desire to marry Karen is strong, very strong, but she can't do it without hurting Doc, which is pretty much the sole reason she and Karen are not engaged now. Natalie feels she cannot have what she wants without hurting those she cares for, and is unwilling to deliberately hurt either Doc or Karen, both of whom she has become somewhat reliant on. As Jasmine noted, she has stopped going out and partying as much, and spends less time drunk or high then she did. Granted, her heroin use has shot up (no pun intended) since becoming physically involved with Doc as she has to deal with flashbacks and feelings of defilement after sex a lot. Natalie probably loves Doc and Karen a similar amount, but her physical desires are for women, and her flashbacks to being raped are not helping her deal with Doc. This was not planned when the relationships started, but sometimes characters go their own way, whatever their players want.

                                        Jasmine's trip was largely her player's work (much as Sebastian is Doc's) though the Guang stuff was information I fed her player and I was of course consulted before a Charm was added to Jasmine's repertoire. I think it is pretty awesome myself.

                                        A quick summery of what happened, we covered two days. The group went to the TV station using Natalie's credentials, and found reels of the show. They also met a lovely reporter who charmed pretty much everyone. Oracle revealed a tall marionette like skeleton in a long white coat, white hat, and with white hair dropped the reels off, with an illusion of humanity. She also apparently filmed it. The reporter Becky Franklin got them a couple names to look for, the then manager and receptionist who accepted the tape and later died under mysterious circumstances. They copied the reels, and discovered they could not see or hear them...except the Little Prince, Simon's Geist...a child. Chandra, when using Auspex, could hear it, but not clearly. The next day, to find out when kids were watching it, Alexia and Karen, with Simon in the trunk, tried to drive near some schools Simon could Boneyard. Driving slowly around playgrounds in antique cars draws attention, and the guileless Alexia near got arrested but for Karen's help. They returned with Karen driving her car and got a time. Predictably, only the Little Prince could hear it or see it. Then they played card/drinking games with monopoly money, because it was game night.

                                        OK well next update will probably cover two sessions for Doc and Jasmine will be back to reporting her experiences with the group. We had the whole jolly band of outlaws there tonight and while we did not make major story advances, the information on Candle Cove was discussed and disseminated, and plans were made for the next step using the information gleaned two weeks ago. Also some tentative plans for getting back in the saddle on the DeCastro ghost issue, and some discussions of Jasmine's uncle in Tiawan. Also, Doc and Simon make a IED.

                                        I'll see you then.
